toṉmaic cemmoḻi
tamiḻ
pi. irāmanātaṉ ka.mu., ca.i.
patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam



nūṟ kuṟippu



poruḷaṭakkam

aṇinturai
tamiḻ muntu cemmoḻi eṉpatai nilaināṭṭum vakaiyil ataṉ toṉmaiyaiyum paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappukaḷaiyum ēraṇa neṟippaṭi eṭuttu viḷakki aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa 'toṉmaic cemmoḻi tamiḻ eṉṉum innūl cemmoḻi paṟṟiyum tamiḻiṉ etirkālam paṟṟiyum palvēṟu pulaṉaṅkaḷait tāṅki yuḷḷatu.
nūlāciriyar pi. irāmanātaṉ moḻiyiyal, varalāṟu, akaḻvāyvu mutaliya tuṟaikaḷil āḻaṅkāṟpaṭṭavar; tamiḻiyam taḻaittaṟku aṭippaṭaiyāṉa eḻuttup paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭiruppavar. 'cintuveḷit tol tamiḻ nākarikam A New Account of the History and Culture of the Tamils' eṉṉun tamiḻ, āṅkila āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻuti veḷi yiṭṭirukkum avar, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar avarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ talaimaiyai nāṭṭum coṟkaḷ' eṉṉun talaippil 'centamiḻc celviyil 1977-80il eḻuti vanta toṭarkaṭṭuraikaḷai (talaimait tamiḻ eṉṉum peyaril tamiḻmaṇ patippakam taṉi nūlāka veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷa tu)"Nostratics : The Light from Tamil According to Devaneyan '' eṉṉum peyaril āṅkilattil moḻiyākkam ceytu paṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷiṉ kavaṉattai īrttiruppavar; pāvāṇar peritum pārāṭṭum varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñarkaḷāṉa vi. ār. irāmaccantiraṉār, piṭi cīṉivācaṉār ākiyōr Originad Spread of Tamils ' eṉavum His - ory of the Tamils ' eṉavum āṅkilattil eḻutiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷukku irāmanātaṉ ceytuḷḷa tamiḻākkamāṉa tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum', 'tamiḻar varalāṟu' eṉṉum nūlkaḷum, cōviyattu aṟiñar alekcāṇṭar kāntiratāv eḻutiya mupperuṅkaṭaṟputirkaḷ (Riddles of Three Oceans ) eṉṉum āyvu nūlil kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭuk kōṭpāṭṭukku araṇ ceyyum vakaiyil amaintuḷḷa intiyap peruṅkaṭal paṟṟiya ataṉ iṭaippakutiyiṉ tamiḻākkamāṉa avartam 'tamiḻa nākarikamum cintuveḷi nākarikamum - teṉperuṅkaṭal āyvukaḷ' eṉṉum nūlum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka vāyilāka veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.
avvaricaiyil oṉṟāṉa innūl tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭattiṉ iṉṟaiya ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ' eṉpatu mutal 'tamiḻiṉ etirkālam - oru tolainōkku' eṉpatu īṟāṉa ēḻu iyalkaḷait tāṅkiyuḷḷatu.
kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷiṉ paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cevvilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟum ammoḻikaḷaic cemmoḻikaḷ eṉṟum aḻaitta airōppiyarkaḷ cīṉam, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpiyam ākiya moḻikaḷaiyum avvāṟē karutiyatōṭu, 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil veḷivanta paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ kirēkka ilattīṉa camaṟkirutat tol ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkuc camamāṉa nilaiyil - cila kūṟukaḷil avaṟṟaiyum viñcum nilaiyil uḷḷatai uṇarntatum - 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattilēyē tamiḻaic cemmoḻiyākak koṇṭaṉa rēṉum, tamiḻ aṟiñar perumakkaḷum amaippukaḷ cilavum tamiḻaic cem moḻiyāka ēṟṟut takka muṟaiyil pōṟṟumāṟu kural eḻuppiyapōtilum, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkeṉa akkaṟai yōṭu poruḷutavi ceytu ūkkuvitta intiya aracu tamiḻaik kaṇṭu koḷḷavē illai.
tamiḻnāṭṭil nayaṉmaik kaṭciyilum pērāyak kaṭciyilum mutaṉmai peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ tamiḻarallātār ātalāl akkaṭcikaḷiṉ āṭcik kālattil tamiḻaic cemmoḻiyāka ēṟṟu aṟivikkumāṟu intiya aracukkuk kōrikkaitāṉum vaittilar; makkaḷ amaippukaḷum kaṇṭu koḷḷavillai. (tamiḻarkaḷ cilar poṟuppēṟpiṉum annilaiyē toṭarntatu.) eṉṉum varalāṟṟu uṇmaiyai irāmanātaṉ kūrntu nōkki innūlil tōṟṟuvāyilēyē teḷivākak kūṟiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappu naṭuvam mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa eḻuvakaip paṇikaḷum teḷivuṟa eṭutturaikkap paṭṭiruppatōṭu, tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpatu kuṟittu kaṭanta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka mutalē aṟiñarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya karutturaikaḷum, amaippukaḷ eḻuppiya kōrikkaikaḷum, piṟavum tokuttaḷikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kālṭuvel kālam varai tamiḻiya (Tamulian ) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē vaḻaṅki vanta peyarait tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiyavar avarē eṉpataik kuṟippiṭum irāmanātaṉ, tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccait tiripu vaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatait teḷivuṟuttuvatōṭu ē. ci. paruṉel intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil ( 1872) kumārilapaṭṭiriṉ tantiravārttikattil kaṇṭa 'appaṭit tamiḻ maṟṟum piṟamoḻikaḷil' eṉap poruḷpaṭum 'tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva' eṉṉum vācakattaip paṭumōcamāka 'āntra tirāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva eṉṟu acciṭṭattaiyum at tavaṟāṉa vācakattaiyē kālṭuvel, teṉkōṉā pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar eṉpataiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭukiṟār. aṉṟiyum itu tavaṟāṉatu eṉpatai intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri 42 ām tokutiyil (1913) pi.ṭi. cīṉivācaṉār teḷivuṟutti niṟuviya piṉṉum, ''kumāril paṭṭar tamiḻaiyum teluṅkaiyum oru cērak kuṟittār' eṉṟu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ palar tavaṟākavē eḻuti varuvataic cuṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tolkāppiyamum kaḻaka (caṅka) ilakkiyaṅkaḷum veḷippaṭātirunta kālattilēyē ellicutturaiyum kālṭuvelum tam ārāycci vaṉmai yāl tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum piṟa ciṟappukaḷaiyum kaṇṭuraittataiyum ap paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷ veḷivantu paraviya nilaiyil tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpatai moḻiyiyal vallunarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koṇṭataiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭum āciriyar, kaḻakam paṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappukaḷ kuṟittum paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷa karuttukaḷait tokuttut tantiruppatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.
cemmoḻit tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip piṟamoḻikaḷil peyarttal eṉṉum pakutiyil, ilakkiyaṅkaḷai moḻipeyarppatil uḷḷa iṭarppāṭukaḷaik kūṟappukuntu, 'tirukkuṟaḷai moḻipeyarttal eṉṉum nūlil kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷai āṅkilattil moḻipeyarppatil uḷḷa cikkalkaḷai viḷakkum pakutiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu moḻipeyarppup paṇiyil īṭupaṭuvōriṉ taṉik kavaṉattiṟku uriyatākum.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaic cintuveḷi nākarika akaḻvāyvu, annākarika eḻuttāyvu uḷḷiṭṭa piṟa aṟivup pulaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇainta āyviṉ mūlam niṟuvuvatum palvēṟu aṟiñarkaḷiṉ āyvuraikaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkiyiruppatum āciriyariṉ parantupaṭṭa nūlaṟivaiyum nuṇmatiyaiyum teḷḷat teḷiya veḷippaṭuttuvaṉavākum.
tamiḻ, tamiḻarp paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaik koccaippaṭutti eḻutuvataiyē vaḻakkamākak koṇṭōr cilar, 'kaṇṭam aḷaviṟkup periya nilapparappu etuvum ekkālattilum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyatillai' eṉṟu iṟṟai aṟiviyal niṟuviyuḷḷatait talaikkīṭākak koṇṭu, kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu paṟṟi nūl eḻutiyōr aṉaivaraiyum tākki ēḷaṉam ceyvataic cuṭṭik kāṭṭum āciriyar, ki.mu. 8000ai oṭṭiya paṉiyūḻi iṟutiyil kaṭal maṭṭam uyarntu ēṟattāḻa irunūṟu munnūṟu kal aḷaviṟkuk karainilappakuti (kaṇṭattiṭṭu - Continental Shelf) kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiviṭṭatu paṟṟiyum, akkaṭaṟkōḷukku muṉpu kaṇṭat tiṭṭup pakuti vaḻiyākat tamiḻiya moḻi pēcunar cila pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē teṉṉintiyāvil iruntu pala pakutikaḷukkum paraviyirukkalām eṉpataṟku aṟiviyalāriṉ iṉṟaiya muṭivukaḷ uṭaṉpaṭuvatai mēṟpaṭiyār kaṇṭukoḷḷātatu kuṟittu nayamākak kaḻaṟukiṟār;
"uraiyāciriyarkaḷ kuṟitta 49 nāṭukaḷum cērntu kaṇṭam aḷaviṟku iruntirukka vēṇṭiyatē illai. muṉpattiyil kūṟiyatu pōlak kaṇṭat tiṭṭup pakuti kaṭaluḷ muḻkiyataiyē kaḻaka nūlkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭalkōḷ ceytikaḷ kūṟuvatākak koṇṭāl iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippu kaḷukkuk kaḻaka ilakkiyak kaṭalkōḷ ceyti muṟṟilum icaivatēyākum" 
eṉṟu teḷivuṟuttukiṟār. itu toṭarpāka nām mēlum cilavaṟṟai eṇṇip pārkkalām.
kaṇṭam eṉṟavuṭaṉ āciyā, āppirikkā mutaliyaṉa pōṉṟa tākavē eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. āṉāl intiyā iṉṟu tīvakkuṟai ( tīpakaṟpam ) eṉṟum tuṇaik kaṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭālum paratakaṇṭam (parata kaṇṭē ) eṉṟum nāvalan tīvu (camput tīvu ) eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum muntaiya vaḻakku nōkkat takkatu. avvāṟē nāṭu eṉpataiyum cīṉā, intiyā pōṉṟatākak karuta vēṇṭiyatillai. tamiḻnāṭṭaiyē eṭuttuk koṇṭālum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ eṉṟum, toṇṭai nāṭu, koṅkunāṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟuvaṉa uḷa. ollaiyūr nāṭu, kāṭṭu nāṭu, kōṉāṭu, paṟampu nāṭu, peṇṇai nāṭu, mukkāvaṉāṭu, vēṅkaṭanāṭu eṉpaṉa puṟanāṉūṟu cuṭṭum nāṭukaḷuṭ cila.
ēṟattāḻac ceṉṉai mānakara aḷavē koṇṭa ciṅkappūr iṉṟum oru taṉināṭāka uḷḷatu; nakara nāṭu (City State) eṉappaṭukiṟatu. vāttikaṉum ( Vatican City ) attakaiyatē. āttirēliyā iyaṟkai yamaippāl oru kaṇṭam eṉṟālum aracāṭci nilaiyaip poṟuttu atu oru nāṭēyām.
paṇṭu kumariyakattu ēḻteṅka nāṭu ēḻmaturai nāṭu mutaliyaṉavāka ēḻēḻ nāṭukaḷ eṉappaṭuvaṉavē nāṟpattu oṉpatu nāṭukaḷāka uṟaḻntu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōr ēḻnāṭu eṉpatu ēḻu kōṭṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa oru maṇṭilam allatu ēḻu vaṭṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa oru māvaṭṭam pōlvatu eṉalām.
kaṭaṟkōḷāl kumarināṭṭai iḻantamaiyiṉ "aṅṅaṉamākiya nilakkuṟaikkuc cōḻanāṭṭellaiyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum, cēramāṉ nāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamum eṉṉum ivaṟṟai iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ" eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟṟum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum.
ivvāṟṟāṉ aṟiviyalār (kaṇṭattiṭṭu) eṉṟum kaṭal kōṭpaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟum kumarinilap parappaik kumarināṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu -- kumarikkaṇṭam eṉṟē kūṟuvatu kūṭa -- aṉṟum iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum nūl vaḻakkiṟkum evvāṟṟāṉum ovvātataṉṟu eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum.
iṉip 'paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟoṭu' eṉat toṭaṅkum cilappatikārap pakutiyāl pāṇṭiyaṉ vaṭaticaikkaṇ kaṅkaiyāṟṟaiyum paṉimalaiyaiyum taṉatu āṭcikkuriyaṉavākak koṇṭatu. teṉṟicaikkaṇ kaṭalkōḷāl tāṉ iḻanta paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟukkum kumarimalaikkum īṭākavē eṉpatu teḷiyap paṭutalāl, teṉpāl kumari nilattiṉ nīṭciyāṉatu vaṭapāl pāṇṭi nāṭṭukkum paṉimalaikkum iṭaippaṭṭa tolaiviṉ aḷaviṉatu (ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅkal) eṉa ūkikkalām.
āṉāl iṟṟai nilaiyil aṟiviyal tuṟaikaḷiṉ koḷḷa iṭantarāmaiyāl cilappatikāram kūṟiyāṅkup pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉ iḻanta teṉṟicaiyāṟṟukkum malaikkum īṭāka vaṭaticaiyāṟṟaiyum malaiyaiyuṅ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, avviraṇṭun tavirnta nāṭṭukku īṭāka 'malitiraiyūrntu eṉat toṭaṅkum kalittokait taravum mēṟkaṇṭa aṭiyārkku nallār uraiyum kūṟiyāṅkuc cēra cōḻa nāṭukaḷiṉ ōrōr pakutiyaik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ aḷaviṉatē eṉṟum iyaipuṟak koḷḷutal cālum.
āyiṉum, ñāla mutaṉmoḻikku aṇukkamāṉatu eṉappaṭum tamiḻukkum, kaṭanta 10000 āṇṭukaḷāka ulakiṉ piṟa pakuti makkaḷōṭu yātoru toṭarpum iṉṟiyirunta āttirēliyap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpukaḷaiyum ūṉṟi nōkkiṉ, mēṟpaṭi ūkam muṟṟilum poruḷaṟṟatu eṉṟu puṟantaḷḷiviṭa muṭiyātu. itu niṟka!
cintuveḷi nākarikam tamiḻaratē eṉpatu ñālamutaṉmoḻi paṟṟiya moḻinūl vallunarkaḷiṉ palvēṟu āyvukaḷ mutal, aṇmaiyil mayilāṭutuṟaiyai aṭutta cempiyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa putiya kaṟkālak karuviyākiya kaṟkōṭariyil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷ īṟāka uḷḷa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ mūlam niṟuvap peṟṟut tamiḻiṉ toṉmai mēlum valiyuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tol tirāviṭam (Proto Dravidian ) eṉpatu paḻantamiḻē yaṉṟic cilar kūṟuvatu pōl vēṟaṉṟu eṉṉum pāvāṇar karuttu vaḻi niṉṟu "innūlil tol tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭuvaṉavellām uṇmaiyil paḻantamiḻaiyē kuṟippatākak koḷka'' eṉṟu āciriyar teḷivuṟuttiyiruppatu naṉṟu.
ippoḻutu palar koṇṭuḷḷa (tavaṟāṉa) karutukōḷiṉpaṭi tol tirāviṭa moḻi pēcunar tāyakam vaṭakiḻakku īrāṉ pakuti eṉṟum aṅkiruntu avarkaḷ intiyā teṉṉāṭu ilaṅkai eṉṟu ki.mu. 3000 aḷavil teṟku nōkki iṭam peyarntu ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟum kūṟum 'tirāviṭar iṟakkak koḷkaikku nērmāṟākat toltirāṭa moḻi pēcunar vaṭakku nōkkip paraviṉar eṉṉum 'tirāviṭar ēṟṟak koḷkaikku ñāṉappirakācar - tēvanēyaṉ kōṭpāṭu' eṉac ciṟappup peyar cūṭṭiyiruppatu peritum naṉṟu.
kumarikkaṇṭam paṟṟiyum, cintuveḷi nākarikam paṟṟiyum takka mēṟkōḷkaḷuṭaṉ irāmanātaṉ kūṟum ceytikaḷ ellām avartam muntaiya nūlkaḷilum virivākak kūṟappaṭṭiruppaṉavēyāyiṉum cem moḻi eṉṉum nilaiyil tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum ciṟappaiyum valivuṟa eṭutturaikkum vakaiyil innūlilum iṭam peṟṟiruppatu takkatē.
intiyāvil uḷḷa tamiḻiya, intō āriya, muṇṭā moḻi kaḷiṭaiyē potumaik kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, inti mutaliya vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ intō āriya moḻikku uriyaṉavalla eṉṟum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟum māṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum ammāṟupāṭṭut taṉmaikaḷ peritum intō āriyam allāta moḻikaḷaic cārntavai eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil ( irukku vētam uṭpaṭak) kaṇicamāṉa aḷavukkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷum tamiḻiyak kūṟukaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum kūṟum vayavar villiyam cōṉcu, kālṭuvel, parō, emaṉō ākiya moḻiyiyal aṟiñarkaḷiṉ āyvuraikaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭit tamiḻiṉ ilakkaṇa amaitiyum tamiḻc coṟkaḷum camaṟkirutam uḷḷiṭṭa intiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittilum paraviyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatai viḷakkiyiruppatōṭu, pāvāṇariṉ āyvuraikaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi aruḷi, aracēntiraṉ āyvuraikaḷum avaṟṟaip peritum valiyuṟuttuvatai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tamiḻiṉ etirkālam paṟṟit tolainōkkākak kūrnturaikkum nūliṉ iṟutippakuti tamiḻuṇarvāḷar ovvoruvarum - kuṟippāka iḷaiya talaimuṟaiyiṉar uṉṉippākak kavaṉattiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatākum. 
ulaka aḷavil kirēkkam, ilattīṉam mutaliya ēṉaic cemmoḻikaḷ pōlātu toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟukāṟum perumpālum citaiviṉṟi vaḻaṅki varum cemmoḻiyākiya tamiḻai araic camaṟkirutak kalavai moḻiyākka 13 ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavilēyē toṭaṅkappaṭṭa naccu muyaṟci veṟṟi mukaṭṭai eṭṭuñ cūḻalil nilaimai talaikīḻāka māṟiviṭṭa tēṉum, kālṭuvel ūṉṟiya vittu maṉōṉmaṇīyam cuntaraṉār, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷār mutaliya aṟiñar perumakkaḷ āṟṟiya arumpaṇi kaḷālum araciyal kumukāya iyakkaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ eḻucciyālum tamiḻp pātukāppum vaḷarcciyum ālamaramāka urukkoṇṭaṉavēṉum payiṟṟu moḻi nilaiyilum āṭci moḻi nilaiyilum tamiḻukku eṉṟō ceytirukka vēṇṭiya paṇikaḷ iṉṟuvarai oḻuṅkākac ceyyap peṟāmai yāl toyvu nilai uḷḷatu eṉpatu naṉku teḷivuṟuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
ulakamayam - paṉṉāṭṭu vaṇikam eṉṉum ceyaṟpāṭṭiṉāl ulakil āṅkilat tavirap piṟamoḻikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa pēriṭaraik kūṟumiṭattup palvēṟu nāṭṭavarkkum nērntuḷḷa pattu vakaiyāṉa māperuṅkēṭupāṭukaḷ, mapuveṉ, kirenōpiḷ pōlum moḻiyiyal aṟiñarkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu viṇṭuraikkappaṭuḷḷaṉa.
intiyāvil piṟamoḻikku illāmal tamiḻukku maṭṭum uḷḷa uṭpakaikaḷākat tēvaiyiṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaik kalappatum koccai vaḻakku iḻivaḻakkukaḷai eḻuttu naṭaiyil pukuttuvatum ākiya 
tamiḻppakaivar ceyal ; eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṉṟa peyaril tamiḻukkuḷḷa ciṟanta varivaṭivattaic citaittut tamiḻai aḻikkat tuṭikkum muḻumakkaḷ ceyal; tolkāppiyam, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil kaivaittu mūlap pāṭattaic citaittum mūlanūl amaippai māṟṟiyum veḷiyiṭum naccup pōkku; ākiya mupperun tīṅkukaḷ cuṭṭik kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu.
ulakamayac curaṇṭaliṉ oru kūṟākavē poruḷiyal paṇpāṭu ākiyavaṟṟaiyum cērttu moḻikaḷum āṅkilattāl aḻikkappaṭuvatāl moḻiyaḷavil maṭṭum muyaṟcikaḷai mēṟkoḷvatu niḻaluṭaṉ pōrāṭuvatē eṉṟum, marapuvaḻip poruḷiyalai vaḷarppatum ulakamayamākkaliṉ palvēṟu nācakārak kūṟukaḷai etirppatumē uṇmaiyāṉa kaḻuvāy eṉṟum, aracukaḷum kaṭcikaḷum mēlai nāṭṭup peru mutalāḷiyakkuḻumaṅkaḷukku aṭimaiyākiviṭṭa nilaiyil avaṟṟai nampip payaṉillaiyātalāl makkaḷ viḻippuṇarvutāṉ vaḻi eṉṟum, ovvoru tēyattavarum tattam moḻiyiṉ pātukāppukkup pōrāṭa vēṇṭiya nilai iruppatāl tamiḻp pātukāppāḷarkaḷ aṇṭai ayal mānilattavaroṭum kalantu ceyaṟpaṭṭu ulaka mayamākkattālum āṅkilattālum intiya moḻikaḷ aṉaittum aḻivukku uḷḷāvatait tavirkka muyala vēṇṭum eṉṟum karuttukaḷ teḷivuṟa eṭutturaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟṟāṉ nūliṉ iṟuvāy oru māperum makkaḷ pōrāṭṭattiṟku muṉmoḻivatāka amaintuḷḷatu.
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvaiyoṭṭi avartam nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum maṟupatippāka orucēra veḷiyiṭṭa tōṭu, taṉik kaṭṭuraikaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum tēṭit tokuttup pala tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭup parappiya perumaikku uriyavarum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷarumāṉa tamiḻmoḻik kāvalar kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ avarkaḷ pāvāṇar nūl veḷiyīṭṭait toṭarntu pāvāṇar karuttukaḷukku araṇ ceyyum vakaiyilāṉa appātturaiyāriṉ nūlkaḷ, na.ci.kantaiyāviṉ nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum muḻumaiyāka veḷiyiṭṭu aruntamiḻp paṇiyait toyviṉṟit toṭarntu varuvatu peritum pārāṭṭi makiḻat takkatu.
tamiḻmaṇ patippaka veḷiyīṭukaḷāṉa pi. irāmanātaṉ nūlvaricaiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa innūl tamiḻiṉ palvakaic ciṟappu kaḷaiyum eṭutturaittu valiyuṟuttuvatōṭu avaṟṟaik kuṟittup paṉṉāṭṭu moḻi yiyal vallunarkaḷum piṟaruṅ kūṟiyuḷḷa karuttumaṇi kaḷait tāṅkiyuḷḷa peṭṭakamākavum tamiḻp pātukāppuk kēṭaya mākavum amaintuḷḷatu.
- iṟaikkuruvaṉār

muṉṉurai
cemmoḻit tamiḻ cārpāṉa ceyti kaḷaik koṇṭu innūl veḷivarukiṟatu. vaḻakkam pōla itaṉaiyum eḻuta eṉṉai ūkkuvitta tamiḻmaṇ patippaka iḷavaḻakaṉārukku eṉatu naṉṟi.
tiṟamāṉa pulamaiyum, nuṇmāṇuḻai pulamum, matukaiyum uṭaiya tamiḻaṟiñar iṟaikkuruvaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa aṇinturaikkum naṉṟiyuṭai yēṉ. avar eṉṉaip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷavaṟṟai eṉpāl uḷḷa aṉpālum eṉṉai ūkkuvikkavum kūṟiya tākak karutukiṟēṉ. aṇinturaikkāka nūl muḻu vataiyum paṭittuḷḷatuṭaṉ āṅkāṅku tēvaiyāṉa tiruttaṅkaḷaic ceytu eṉatu naṭaiyaic ceppaṉiṭṭuḷḷār. ataṟkum naṉṟi eḷitil orē iṭattil kiṭṭātaṉavum, iṉṟaiya paltuṟai aṟiviyaṟpula vallunarkaḷ maṟukkoṇātavaiyumāṉa ceytikaḷ cilapala vaṟṟai ōriṭattil tokuttup palariṉ pārvaikkum koṇṭu varuvatē innūliṉ nōkkamākum.
pi. irāmanātaṉ

patippurai
kālṭuvel 1856 il tamatu "teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇattai veḷiyiṭṭatumē ulaka moḻiyiyalaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ toṉmai, muṉmai, cemmai mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, ñāla mutaṉmoḻikkut tamiḻ mika neruṅkiyatāka niṟuvappaṭa vāyppu iruppataiyum uṇarntaṉar. eṉiṉum "cemmoḻi' eṉa oru moḻiyai ēṟṟiṭat toṉmoḻiyāyiṉum ammoḻi ilakkiyam ciṟantatākavum, taṉittaṉmai vāyntatākavum, piṟamoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷai aṭiyoṟṟi uruvākātatumākavum irukka vēṇṭum. 1850 kkup piṉṉartāṉ tolkāppiyam mutalāṉa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭap paṭṭaṉa. 1900 aḷavilēyē moḻi aṭippaṭaiyilum cari, ilakkiya aṭippaṭaiyilum cari, tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē eṉpatai ulaka aṟiñarulakam ēṟṟuk koṇṭatu. intiya naṭuvaṇ aracu aḷavil, tamiḻmoḻi cemmoḻi eṉa mikak kālam tāḻntu 2004 il tāṉ ēṟkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkup palarum kāraṇamāvar - tamiḻarkaḷākiya nām uṭpaṭa. cemmoḻik kōrikkai varalāṟṟai innūl iyal iraṇṭil kāṇka.
kālap paḻamaiyāl vairam pāyntatu! 
nākarikattiṉ nāṟṟaṅ kālatu 
emmoḻikkum atu īṭiṇaiyaṟṟa 
cemmoḻi ulakac cintaṉaikkellām
ūṟṟāyt tulaṅkum uṇmaiyiṉ paiñcuṉai! 
eṉṟār pāvēntar. pāvēntariṉ kaṉavai naṉavākki tamiḻaic cemmoḻiyāka naṭuvaṇ aracu ēṟṟu aṟivikkac ceyta māṇpumiku tamiḻaka mutalvar kalaiñar avarkaḷukkut tamiḻar aṉaivarum naṉṟikkaṭaṉ paṭṭavarkaḷ.
cemmoḻit tamiḻttiṭṭattil ēṟkeṉavē toṭaṅki 2005 iliruntu ūkkattuṭaṉ naṭattivarum paṇikaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷai innūliṉ palvēṟu iyalkaḷil kāṇalām. cemmoḻit tamiḻppaṇikaḷai, naṭuvaṇ, mānila aracukaḷiṉ tiṭṭam eṉṟu karutāmal kaṟṟaṟinta tamiḻar ovvoruvarum taṅkaḷatu paṅkaḷippaiyum ātaravaiyum tara vēṇṭum.
paṭippaṭiyāka cemmoḻit tamiḻt tiṭṭattiṉ paṇikaḷ virivākkappaṭṭu mēlum pala paṇikaḷ toṭaṅkum eṉpatil aiyamillai. avvāṟu toṭaṅku muṉṉar palatuṟai aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu cemmoḻit tamiḻt tiṭṭa ciṟappu naṭuvamum naṭuvaṇ, mānila aracukaḷum piṉvarum eṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum karutiṭa vēṇṭum.
1. ñāla mutaṉmoḻi āyvukaḷukkut tamiḻē vaḻikāṭṭa vallatu eṉpatai iyal 4 viḷakkukiṟatu. ittuṟaiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻaka moḻiyiyal āyvu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum taṉippaṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷum toṭarpu koḷvatai ūkkuvikka vēṇṭum.
2. tamiḻicaiyē ulaka icai vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭai eṉṟu 'karuṇāmirta cākaram' eṉṟa taṉatu ārāycci nūlil uṟutippaṭutti yuḷḷār aṟiñar āpirakām paṇṭitar. iṉṟu tamiḻicai piṟanta nāṭṭilēyē urimaiyiḻantu nalintuviṭṭatu. icai nūlkaḷ ellām moḻimāṟṟam peṟṟu vaṭamoḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻicai maṟumalarcci peṟa vēṇṭum. tamiḻicai nūlkaḷait tamiḻākkam ceytu tamiḻōṭu cērkka vēṇṭum.
3. intiyāvilēyē tamiḻ nāṭṭiltāṉ neṭituyarnta kōpuraṅ kaḷōṭu kūṭiya kōvilkaḷ mikuti. ivai tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaṭak kalaikkum ciṟpak kalaikkum nēraṭiyāṉa cāṉṟu, eṉṟālum - kōyil kaṭṭaṭakkalai maṟṟum ciṟpak kalai paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻil oṉṟu kūṭa illai. attaṉaip paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷum vaṭa moḻikkuc ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟait tamiḻil moḻipeyarttu tamiḻvaḷam perukka vēṇṭum.
4. tamiḻ maruttuvam - tamiḻukkuriya attuṇait toṉmaic ciṟappukaḷaiyum koṇṭatu. paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ aṟivukkuc cāṉṟāka amaivatu. piṟkālattil cittarkaḷāl vaḷarkkappaṭṭu 'citta maruttuvam eṉṟāṉatu. tamiḻ maruttuvattaik koṇṭu vaḷarntavaitāṉ intiyāvil luḷḷa maṟṟa mūlikai maruttuva muṟaikaḷ. iṉṟu tamiḻ maruttuvam ōraḷavu maṟumalarcci peṟṟu varukiṟatu. tamiḻ maruttuvam virivāka ārāyappaṭṭu - paḻantamiḻ maruttuvac cuvaṭikaḷilum - nūlkaḷilumiruntu tamiḻ maruttuvak kalaik kaḷañciyam' uruvākkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
5. eṇṇum - eḻuttum vāḻum uyirkaḷukkuk kaṇkaḷ eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. mutaṉ mutal eṇkaḷai uruvākkip payaṉpaṭuttiyavarkaḷ paḻantamiḻarkaḷē. iṉṟaiya aṟiviyal vaḷarccikkum - 'kaṇiṉik kalaic ceḻippukkum avveṇkaḷē aṭippaṭai. paṇṭait tamiḻakattōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya arapu nāṭṭu vaṇikar mūlam avveṇkaḷ ulakeṅkum paravi - iṉṟu arapu eṇ' eṉṟa peyaril vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. eṇ kaṇitamuṟai ārāyappaṭṭu - itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṇṭupiṭippu eṉpatai ulakaṟiyac ceyya vēṇṭum.
6. intiya aracu 'cemmoḻikaḷukku vaḻaṅkum attaṉaic ciṟappukaḷaiyum naṉmaikaḷaiyum kālantāḻttātu cemmoḻit tamiḻukkum urittākka vēṇṭum. piṉvarum ceyalpāṭukaḷum itil aṭaṅkum:
a) piṟa mānilaṅkaḷilum ayalnāṭukaḷilum uḷḷa palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷil tamiḻ irukkaikaḷ niṟuviṭa niti utavi tarutal
ā cemmoḻit tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppukaḷ veḷivara niti utavit tiṭṭam (tamiḻiliruntu piṟa moḻikaḷilum, piṟamoḻikaḷil liruntu tamiḻilum)
i) tamiḻc cemmoḻi cārnta ciṟanta karuvi nūlkaḷai mūlamākavō, moḻipeyarppākavō veḷiyiṭum aṟiñarkaḷukkum niṟuvaṉaṅ kaḷukkum niti utavit tiṭṭam.
ī) vaḷarntuvarum iḷaiya talaimuṟai cemmoḻit tamiḻ marapaiyum, paṇpāṭṭaiyum aṟivataṟku utavum vakaiyil, at tamiḻ marapucār veḷiyīṭukaḷai eḷiya naṭaiyil vaṇṇappaṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷik koṇṭuvara niti utavit tiṭṭam. caṅkat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟukkum cempatippukaḷ (Critical Editions) veḷivarat takka ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum.
u) ayal nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷākiya kamil cuvalapil, jārj el. hārṭ, ār. ī. āṣar, kurō, cāṉpōrṭu sṭīvar, harālṭu ṣipmāṉ, sṭepāṉ leviṭ, īvā vil taṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ payaṉpeṟum vakaiyil nam nāṭṭu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil '' varukaip pērāciriyar " ( Visiting Professorship ) paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ ; intiyāviṟku vantu pōvataṟkāṉa payaṇak kaṭṭaṇa utavit tokaikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai ēṟpaṭuttutal.
7. (a) ilakkiyap paḻamaikkum, aṟiviyal putumaikkum ēṟṟa colvaḷam koṇṭa moḻi tamiḻ. aṟiviyal cārnta toḻiṟ kalviyait tamiḻāl kaṟpikka muṭiyum. ulakaḷavil viraintu vaḷarum aṟiviyal kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷai uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ tamiḻākka muṭiyum. tamiḻ - āṅkilam iraṇṭilum valla antantat tuṟai vallunarkaḷ ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭa ūkkuvittal vēṇṭum. ataṟku ēṟṟa vakaiyil aṟiviyal kalaiccol kaḷañciyamum uruvākkappaṭa vēṇṭum.
(ā) ciṟanta kalaikkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ maruttuvam, aṟiviyal, ciṟuvar kalaik kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavai uruvāka utavi ceytal.
(i) āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarātiyaip poṟuttavarai 1960kaḷil citamparanātaṉār - appātturaiyār tokuttu veḷivanta ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka akarātikkup piṉ ivvakaiyil taramāṉa putu muyaṟcikaḷ
illai. takunta aṟiñarkaḷai ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭutti iṉṟaiya nilaikku ēṟpuṭaiya, ulakattaram vāynta āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarāti tokuttu veḷiyiṭappaṭavēṇṭum.
8. (a) cintu veḷiyil akaḻāyvukaḷ naṭattiya takuti vāynta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷa cintuveḷi āyvu muṭivukaḷai mēlum ārāyntu uṇmai nilaiyai ulakaṟiyac ceyya ēṟkeṉavē cemmoḻit tamiḻ naṭuvam karutiyuḷḷatu ammuyaṟcikaḷait toṭara vēṇṭum.
(ā) tamiḻiṉ toṉmai, tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarccikkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷākiya kipi 600kku muṟpaṭṭa poṟippukaḷ - kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ muḻumaiyāṉa āyvu ; tamiḻar nākarikam , vaṇikam mutaliyavaṟṟukku ātāramāṉa tamiḻakak kaṭal, kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷil akaḻvāyvu ; ākiyavaṟṟai muṉaippuṭaṉ ceyya vēṇṭum.
(9) tamiḻar perum aḷavil vāḻum ayal nāṭukaḷil tūtarkaḷākat tamiḻarkaḷai amarttuvatu annāṭukaḷil tamiḻ vaḷarccikkup pātukāppu araṇāka amaiyum. itu kuṟittut tamiḻaka aracu veḷiyuṟavuttuṟai amaiccakattukkup parinturaikka vēṇṭum. ayalakat tamiḻarkaḷiṭaiyē tamiḻ nilai peṟuvataṟkāṉa palvakai naṭavaṭikkaikaḷaiyum eṭukka vēṇṭum.
ulakiṉ ēṉaiya cemmoḻikaḷ vāḻntu ceḻitta kālattil em. tamiḻmoḻiyum vāḻntatu. iṉṟu ammoḻikaḷ aḻinta nilaiyil iḷamaikuṉṟā uyirppuṭaṉ uḷḷatu nam tāymoḻiyām teḷḷiya tīntamiḻē...
"ulakiṉ talaiciṟantaṉavākak karutappeṟum aṟu cemmoḻikaḷuḷ - uyirppōṭum, ūṭṭattoṭum, cemmāp poṭum, cīroṭum, ciṟappoṭum, tolpaḻantiruvoṭum, kalaṅkaṟāk karuvōṭum, vaḷattōṭum, nalat toṭum, oṭpoṭum, tiṭpoṭum, ulakan tarīiya naṭpōṭum, eṇṇat tiṉikkum iṉiya peṭpōṭum ulavi uvappuṟuttuvatāka, nam teḷḷiya tīntamiḻē iṉṟu - naṉṟu nīṭi niṉṟu nimirnteḻuntu naṭai ciṟantu iyaṅki varukiṉṟamaiyai, mutalil nantamiḻar neñcam niṟakka uṇartal vēṇṭum!'' 
eṉṟa collāyvu aṟiñar aruḷiyiṉ neñcak kūṟṟait tamiḻarkaḷ niṉaikka vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
tamiḻ moḻi, iṉa nāṭṭuṇarvu eṉ elumpōṭum narampōṭum mūḷaiyōṭum, kurutiyōṭum iraṇṭaṟak kalantu ūṟip pōṉatu. atuvē eṉakku uṇavākavum uyirākavum vāḻvākavum āṉatu. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ cemmāppiṉai ulakamelām paṟaicāṟṟum vakaiyil aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ eḻutiya toṉmaic cemmoḻi tamiḻ eṉṉum ivvaruntamiḻ nūliṉai tamiḻ kūṟum ulakukkup putuvaravākak koṭuttuḷḷōm.
innūlukku aṇinturai vaḻaṅkiya pāvāṇar vaḻinilai aṟiñar taṉittamiḻk kuricil, teṉ moḻik kuṭumpa marapiṉ mūttavar tirukkuṟaḷ maṇi iṟaikkuruvaṉārukku neñcam niṟainta naṉṟi. maṟṟum kaṇiṉi ceytu utaviya mu.na. irāmacuppiramaṇiya rācāvum, "kuṭvil celviyum, pārāṭṭukkuriyar.

vīṟuṭaiya cemmoḻi tamiḻmoḻi - ulakam 
vērūṉṟiya nāḷ mutal uyirmoḻi!
vīṟuṭaiya cemmoḻi ... 
māṟupaṭum moḻikaḷaippōl māṟavillai 
maṅkivarum moḻikaḷaippōl maṅkavillai 
vēṟupaṭum moḻikaḷaippōl vēṟākavillai 
vīḻntupaṭum moḻikaḷaippōl vīḻavillai
vīṟuṭaiya cemmoḻi ... 
kūṟupaṭum moḻikaḷaippōl kulaiyavillai 
koñcip pēcum vaḻakkaṟṟuk kumaiyavillai! 
cāṟupaṭṭa maraṅkaḷaippōl cāyavillai 
taraṅkeṭṭa maṉitaraip pōl tāḻavillai!
vīṟuṭaiya cemmoḻi ... 
eṉṉum pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār varikaḷait taṅkaḷ neñca vayalil vitaikkiṟēṉ. viḷaiviṉ payaṉait tamiḻ ulakam koḷḷaṭṭum.
innūl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ illantōṟum irukkavēṇṭiya tamiḻkkāppu nūl vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

iyal 1
tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭattiṉ
iṉṟaiya ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ
mēlai airōppiya nāṭukaḷil paṇṭaik kālattilēyē (ippōtaiya airōppiya moḻikaḷil ilakkiyaṅkaḷ evaiyum tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉṉarē) ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiya kirēkka, ilattīṉ moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic "cevvilakkiyam " Classical literature eṉa airōppiyar aḻaittaṉar; avviru moḻikaḷaiyum "cemmoḻikaḷ" Classical languages eṉṟaṉar. Classical eṉpataṉ aṭippaṭaip poruḷ "mutal taramāṉa / cevviya / cemmaiyāṉa " eṉpatē yākum. avviru moḻikaḷiṉ (ki.mu. 500 - ki.pi. 300 cārnta) ciṟanta paltuṟai ilakkiyaṅkaḷum antanta moḻiyilēyē tāmāka uruvāṉavai; piṟamoḻit tākkattiṉāl alla. avvilakkiyaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷumē piṟṟai airōppiya moḻikaḷukku (āṅkilam, pireñcu, ceru māṉiyam, spāṉiyam, uruciyaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟukku muṉmātiri yāka amaintaṉa.
2. piṉṉar 18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyiliruntu ulakiṉ piṟa pakutikaḷil irunta ittakaiya moḻikaḷaiyum cemmoḻikaḷ eṉa airōppiya moḻiyiyalaṟiñarum ilakkiya aṟiñarum ēṟkalāyiṉar; aḻaikkalāyiṉar. cīṉam, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arapu mutaliyavai 1800 toṭakkattil iruntē ivvāṟu karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ toṉmai yāṉa moḻi eṉa 1816 - liruntutāṉ (pirāṉcisvaiṭ ellis) uṇarappaṭṭatu; 1856-liruntutāṉ (kālṭuvel) ēṟkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl 2000 āṇṭa
'' varai ēṟkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kaḻaka (caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭu, tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutaliyavai - acciṭṭu veḷivanta kālam ki.pi. 1870-1900 āṇṭu kaḷēyākum. kirēkka, ilattīṉ, camaṟkirutat tol ilakkiyaṅkaḷukkuc camamāṉa - cila kūṟukaḷil viñciya - paṇṭait tamiḻ ilakkiyam uḷḷatai uṇarntatum tamiḻaic cemmoḻi eṉa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar 1920 kaḷiliruntē naṭaimuṟaiyil ottuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. intiya aracu1800 -liruntē camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiyavaṟṟaic cemmoḻiyāka "āḷumai naṭaimuṟaippaṭi" (Executive action) ēṟṟuk koṇṭu avaṟṟai aracu nitiyiliruntu celavu ceytu ūkkuvittu vantatu. tamiḻ paṟṟi attakaiya kōrikkaikaḷ (tamiḻum cemmoḻi eṉa aṟiñar ēṟṟa) 1900 - liruntu avvappoḻutu eḻuppappaṭṭu vantaṉa. āṉāl tamiḻakap pakutiyiṉ araciyalatikārattaik kaikkuḷ vaittirunta naṭuvaṅkaḷil alla! tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpataik kaṭanta nūṟāṇṭukaḷil viḷakkiya aṟiñar palar karuttukaḷ, akkāla kaṭṭattil cemmoḻiyāka ēṟka viṭutta kōrikkaikaḷ paṟṟiya varalāṟṟai iyal 2-il kāṇalām)
3. ceṉṉai mākāṇattil 1920 ticampar mātam nayaṉmaik kaṭci āṭcikku vantatu. ceṉṉai mākāṇa aracu 1950 caṉavariyil ceṉṉai mānila aracu āṉatu. 1956 navamparilēyē ceṉṉai mānila aracu 'tamiḻaka aracu' ākiviṭṭatākak karutalām (peyar māṟṟam 1967 -l tāṉ vantatu eṉṟa pōtilum). nayaṉmaik kaṭciyilum pērāyak kaṭciyilum mutaṉmai peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ tamiḻarallātār ākaiyāl tamiḻaic cemmoḻiyāka ēṟka naṭuvaṇaracukku avarkaḷ kōrikkai tāṉum vaittilar. cāmāṉiyat tamiḻaruḷ perumpāṉmaiyiṉar (aṉṟum iṉṟum) tamiḻ, tamiḻar nalaṉaippaṟṟik kavalaippaṭupavar allar; tannalam, kuṭumpa nalam, cātinalam, kaṭci nalam ivaṟṟukku appāṟpaṭṭavai paṟṟi "eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu emakku" eṉṟu iruppavarkaḷ; eṉavē avarkaḷum tām cārntirunta kaṭcit talaivarkaḷiṭam cemmoḻik kōrikkaiyai vaikkavillai. nallūḻ kāraṇamāka 2004 attōparil tamiḻai naṭuvaṇaracu nilaiyil "cemmoḻiyāka ēṟpaḷittatu. 2005 cūlai mutal tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭattai naṭuvaṇaracu tiṟampaṭac ceyalpaṭutti varukiṟatu. ivvaruñceyalukkuk kāraṇamāṉavar aṉaivarum mānila, maiya araciṉarum namatu naṉṟikkuriyarāvar.
4. tamiḻc cemmoḻit tiṭṭac ceyalpāṭṭup poṟuppai maicūriluḷḷa (naṭuvaṇaracu mānta vaḷa amaic cakam : uyarkalvit tuṟaiyiṉ kīḻ ceyalpaṭum) ''intiya moḻikaḷukkāṉa naṭuvaṇaracu niṟuvaṉattiṉ" amaippāṉa ''tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam, atāvatu 
Centre of Excellence for Classical Tamil 
Central Institute of Indian Languages
Manasa gangotri; Mysore 570 006; Karnataka State 
ēṟṟuc cevvaṉē naṭatti varukiṟatu. niṟuvaṉa iyakkunar utaya nārāyaṇaciṅ avarkaḷum tuṇai iyakkunar kē. irāmacāmiyum ivvakaiyil pārāṭṭukkuriyavar. viraivil inniṟuvaṉam ceṉṉaiyiliruntu ceyalpaṭa virukkiṟatu.
5. tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam paltuṟai aṟiñar, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, āyvakaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ kalantāyvu ceytu, piṉvarum ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷai ēṟkeṉavē 2005 cūlai mutal toṭaṅki naṭatti varukiṟatu.
(1) tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiya (caṅka ilakkiya) nūlkaḷ 41 - il ovvoṉṟukkum āyvup patippu veḷi yiṭutal - mutalil mūlam ; piṉṉar uraiyuṭaṉ Critical Editions of Ancient Tamil Works.
ippaṇiyai 21 tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷaik koṇṭu naṭuvam toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu (nāṟpattoru nūlkaḷ paṭṭiyalai iyal mūṉṟil kāṇka.)
(2) kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷaip piṟa moḻikaḷil peyarttu veḷiyiṭutal. (Translation of Ancient Tamil Works.)
kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟukku ēṟkeṉavē āṅkilattil moḻi peyarppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟukku oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa moḻi peyarppukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. tirukkuṟaḷukku āṅkila moḻi peyarppukaḷ cumār muppatu uḷḷaṉa; 1794il laṇṭa ṉil veḷiyāṉa Specimens of Hindoo Literature nūlil eṉ. ī. kiṉṭarsli kuṟaḷ mutal 12 atikāraṅ kaḷiliruntu cila kuṟaḷkaḷai eṭuttu āṅkilappaṭutti veḷi yiṭṭār. 1819 il 120 kuṟaḷkaḷukku virivāṉa viḷakkavuraiyuṭaṉ ellis tam moḻi peyarppai veḷiyiṭṭār. 1840-1886 kāla aḷavil turu (Drew), ilācaras, pōp pōṉṟa pala muḻumai yāṉa āṅkila moḻipeyarppukkaḷ veḷiyāyiṉa. airōppiya moḻikaḷil mutalil ilattīṉil tāṉ 1730 il vīramāmuṉivar kuṟaḷiṉ mutal irupālkaḷai maṭṭum peyarttu veḷiyiṭṭār. kuṟaḷiṉ ēṉaiya airōppiya moḻi ākkaṅkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavai pireñcu - ēriyal (1848); cerumaṉ - kirāl (1856 ākum.
ikkuṟaḷ moḻipeyarppukaḷ ulaka aḷavil perum tākkattai ēṟpaṭuttiṉa (kāṇka iyal 3: tirukkuṟaḷ)
kaḻaka ilakkiya akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷ vēṟu em moḻip pāṭalkaḷilum illāta taṉicciṟappuṭaiyavai eṉpatai ē. kē. irāmāṉucaṉuṭaiya kuṟuntokaiyiṉ arumaiyāṉa āṅkila ākkam 1967 il (The Interior Landscape) veḷivantatum paṉṉāṭṭu ilakkiyavāṇarum teḷḷitiṉ uṇarntaṉar.
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil cilavaṟṟukku ēṟkeṉavē uḷḷa (āṅkila moḻipeyarppukaḷai) aṟiñarkaḷaik koṇṭu āyvu ceytu avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantavaṟṟai naṭuvam viraivil acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭavirukkiṟatu. aṭutta kaṭṭamākap putiya āṅkila moḻi peyarppukaḷum ceyyappaṭum. putiya moḻipeyarppukkāka ovvoru nūlukkum tamiḻ aṟiñar oruvar, moḻi peyarppāḷar oruvar, āṅkila aṟiñar oruvar ākiyōraik koṇṭa mūvar kuḻuvai naṭuvam amaikkum.
piṟamoḻikaḷil : inti, malaiyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, pireñcu mutaliya moḻikaḷilum kaḻaka ilakkiyap peyarppukaḷai naṭuvam viraivil koṇaravirukkiṟatu.
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷ, kuṟippāka vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattilum kāṇaviyalātaṉavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya aṟiñar kūṟṟukkaḷai iyal 3il kāṇalām. kaḻaka ilakkiya nūlkaḷaip piṟa moḻikaḷil peyarppatu paṟṟiyatu iyal 4.
(3) varalāṟṟumuṟait tamiḻilakkaṇam veḷiyiṭutal Historical Grammar of Tamil
tamiḻ oliyaṉiyal, oliyiyal, peyarccolliyal, viṉaic colliyal, iṭaiccoṟkaḷ, toṭarpaṉiyal (Phonetics, Phonology, Noun morphology, Verb morphology, particles. Syntax) ākiya āṟu tuṟaikaḷilum moḻiyilaṟiñar aṟuvaraik koṇṭu āḷukkoru iyalai eḻutuvittu, tokuttu ivvilakkaṇa nūlai naṭuvam veḷiyiṭa virukkiṟatu. kāla varampukaḷ, cemmoḻikkālam kimu. 300 - ki.pi. 600; iṭaikkālam ki.pi. 601-1600; ikkālam ki.pi. 1601 mutal - eṉṟavāṟu amaiyum.
(4) tamiḻiṉ toṉmai : piṟa aṟivuppulaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇainta āyvu : Antiquity of Tamil: an interdisciplinary Approach.
tamiḻ icaiyiyal, kaḻakakkālam cārnta kukaik kal veṭṭukaḷ, kaḻakak kālap pāṉai ōṭukaḷ, kācukaḷ, muttirai kaḷ, naṭukaṟkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil uḷḷa poṟippukaḷ kalveṭṭu kaḷ ivai cārnta āyvukaḷai naṭuvam ūkkuvikkum cintu veḷi nākarika eḻuttāyvukaḷaiyum ūkkuvikkum. putiya akaḻvāyvukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷavum vaḻivakaikaḷ kāṇap paṭum.
paḻantamiḻ icaippāṭalkaḷai icaikkum (90 maṇit tuḷi) kuṟuntakaṭu oṉṟu aṇiyamākiyuḷḷatu.
iṉṟaiya nāstirātik / yūrēciyāṭik āyvāḷarkaḷ, ñāla mutaṉ moḻi āyvāḷarkaḷ, tolliyalāḷar, marapaṇu āyvāḷar ākiyōr pārvaiyil tamiḻiṉ toṉmai paṟṟi iṉṟaiya aṟiñarulakam koṇṭuḷḷa karuttukkaḷai iyal 4il kāṇalām.
(5) tamiḻiṉ vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷ : (ka) kālantōṟum iruntavai (ilakkiya, kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷiṉ paṭi ) maṟṟum (u) iṉṟu tamiḻakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷilum uḷḷavai : Diachronic and synchronic study of Tamil dialects.
ipporuḷ cārnta 1100 nūlkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷai naṭuvam tokuttuḷḷatu. vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc col akarāti vaṭṭāra vaḻakku nilappaṭam (Dialect Atlas) ākiyavaṟṟai uruvākkuvatu nōkkamākum. pala aṭippaṭaikaḷil ituvarait tokutta 40,000 vaṭṭāra vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ (palvēṟu toḻil kaḷukkuriya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ, kumukap pirivukaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷ uṭpaṭa) kaṇiṉiyil ēṟṟappaṭuḷḷaṉa; paṇi toṭarkiṟatu.
(6) intiya moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭaiyē potumai India as a Linguistic Area
itu paṟṟiya āyvukaḷukku utavum vakaiyil tamiḻ - piṟamoḻi akarātikaḷ, piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavai veḷiyiṭappaṭum. paṇṭārakarkaḷ tāmas parō - marrē pārṉcaṉ emeṉō tokutta tirāviṭac coṟpiyal akaramutali Dravidian Etymological Dictionary (1961 / tiruttiya patippu 1984kku iṇaipput tokuti oṉṟai naṭuvam uruvākki varukiṟatu. vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa intō - āriya moḻik kuṭumpam, muṇṭā moḻikkuṭumpam pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ moḻi kaḷil uḷḷa tamiḻiyak kūṟukaḷ paṟṟi ituvarai moḻiyiyal 
aṟiñar kaṇṭuḷḷa muṭivukaḷiṉ curukkattai iyal 5 il kāṇalām.
(7) cemmoḻit tamiḻ āyvu kuṟitta kaṇiṉi nūlakam uruvākkutal Digital library for ancient Tamil Studies:
ivvāyvu cārnta 3800 nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya viḷattaṅkaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttilum urōmaṉ eḻuttilum tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyvuc ceytikaḷait tamiḻilō āṅkilattilō uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ peṟakkūṭiya viḷattak kaḷañciyamum Tamil - English Bilngual Thesaurus uruvāki varukiṟatu. tēvaiyāṉa meṉ poruḷ mutaliyaṉavum aṇiyamākiṉṟaṉa.
(8) cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyattai "āṉlaiṉ" vaḻik kaṟpittal . Online Teaching of Classical Tamil
akappāṭal, puṟappāṭal, ilakkiyam, aṟanūl, ilakkaṇam (ceyyuḷiyal uṭpaṭa) ākiya aintu vakaic kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum mottam 300 maṇinēram ''āṉlaiṉ' vaḻi kaṟpikkat tiṭṭamiṭṭu, paṇi toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.
(9) cemmoḻit tamiḻilakkiyat tokuppaik kaṇiṉiyil uruvākkutal. Corpus development for Classical Tamil works.
cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ 41 iṉ āyvup patippukaḷ veḷiyāṉatum ippaṇi toṭaṅki niṟaivēṟum ; ataṟkāṉa tiṭṭam uruvākiviṭṭatu.
(10) cemmoḻit tamiḻ cār paṭakkāṭcikaḷai uruvākkutal. Visual episodes on Classical Tamil
tamiḻ moḻi, kaḻaka ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam, kalveṭṭiyal, kāciyal, kalai, kaṭṭumāṉam, camayam, tamiḻ ayalnāṭukaḷil paraval pōṉṟa poruṇmaikaḷil ēṟattāḻa 100 paṭakkāṭcikaḷ uruvākkappaṭum. mutaṟkaṇ 13 paṭakkāṭcikaḷ terinteṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyam - 4 tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ - 2 ; tamiḻarkāciyal - 2; tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tolvaravu - 2; kaḻakak kālat tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ / talainakaraṅkaḷ / vaṇika naṭuvaṅkaḷ - 3
6. tamiḻ nāṭṭil palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam kaṭanta ōrāṇṭukku mēlākap pala karuttaraṅkukaḷaiyum āyvaraṅ kaṅkaḷaiyum naṭatti varukiṟatu. annaṭuvattuṭaṉ iṇaintu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷum, tamiḻppaṟṟāḷarum, tamiḻ ilakkiyam, moḻi, paṇpāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ mēmpāṭṭukkum potu makkaḷiṭaiyē ivai paṟṟiya ārvattai ūṭṭuvataṟkum muyalvārkaḷāka
7. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyiṉ aṉaittup poruṇmaikaḷaiyum kuṟittu naṭuvaṇ aracukku aṟivurai taruvataṟku aṟiñar muṉaivar vā.ce.kuḻantaicāmi talaimaiyil vallunar patiṉmaraik koṇṭa 'tamiḻmoḻivaḷarcci āyam Tamil language Promotion Board” oṉṟai naṭuvaṇ aracu amaittuḷḷatu. tamiḻc cemmoḻi aṟiñarkaḷuḷ ciṟantavarkaḷukkum ayal nāṭṭavarkkum, nam nāṭṭavarkkum āṇṭutōṟum cila ciṟappu virutukaḷ vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭamum aṟivikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻc cemmoḻi aṟiñarkaḷil ciṟantavarkaḷukku āṇṭu tōṟum matippuc cāṉṟitaḻum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. iḷam akavai aṟiñarkaḷai ūkkuvikkap paricut tokaikaḷum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. muṉaivar paṭṭattukku mēl nilaiyilāṉa āyvup paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ aintu muṉaivar paṭṭa āyvup paṇiyiṭaṅkaḷ pattu ākiyavaṟṟaiyum āṇṭutōṟum vaḻaṅkuvatāka naṭuvaṇ aracu maṉitavaḷa amaiccakam 14 - 7 - 2005 aṉṟu aṟivittuḷḷatu. viraivil cemmoḻit tamiḻ naṭuvam ceṉṉaiyiliruntu ceyaṟpaṭuvataṟkāṉa ēṟpāṭukaḷai mānila aracum maiya aracum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉa.

iyal 2
''tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē': karutturaikaḷ, kōrikkaikaḷ paṟṟiya
oru varalāṟṟup pārvai
1. tamiḻ taṉimoḻi, teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ (teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, malaiyāḷam mutaliyavai) tamiḻiya moḻik kuṭumpattaic cārntavai eṉpatai mutalil 1816 il niṟuviyavar pirāṉcis vaiṭ ellis . piṉṉar 1828 il pi.ji. pāpiṅṭaṉum 1853 il ār. hoyciṅṭaṉum ikkaruttaik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 1856 il kālṭuvel tamatu "tirāviṭa allatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam" nūlai veḷiyiṭṭu tamiḻ intō airōppiya moḻikaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatākalām eṉṟum ñālamutaṉ moḻikku mika neruṅkiyatāka irukkalām eṉṟum niṟuviṉār. varalāṟṟu moḻiyiyalil annūl ulaka aḷavil mikac cīriya iṭattaip peṟṟiruppatākum. (ēṟkaṉavē coṉṉapaṭi) 1870-1900 kāla aḷavil caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ aṉaittum accil veḷi vantatum paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyam cevvilakkiyam eṉpataiyum tamiḻmoḻi cemmoḻi eṉpataiyum naṭaimuṟaiyil paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarum ēṟpārāyiṉar (naṭuvaṇaracai ēṟkac ceyya 2005 varai āṉataṟkut tamiḻariṉ ūkkak kuṟaivē kāraṇam eṉpataiyum kaṇṭōm. kaṭanta nūṟu āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉap pakarnta aṟiñar cilar kūṟṟukkaḷai ivviyalil kāṇpōm:
2. palkalaikkaḻaka vakuppukaḷil intiyāveṅkum cemmoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutam maṭṭum kaṟṟuttantāl pōtum, uṇṇāṭṭu moḻikaḷ Vernaculars kaṟṟut taravēṇṭām eṉa karcāṉ pirapuviṉ naṭuvaṇaracu 1902ai oṭṭik kaṭṭuppāṭu ceyya muyaṉṟa poḻutu ataṟku etirāṉa parappuraikaḷai vi.kō.cūriya nārāyaṇa cāttiriyār, em.es. pūraṇaliṅkam piḷḷai , lācaras mutaliyavarkaḷ ceytaṉar.
akkāla kaṭṭattil tamatu tamiḻmoḻi varalāṟu (1903) nūlil tamiḻ cemmoḻiyē eṉa piṉvarumāṟu kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttiṉār cāttiriyār :
''vaṭamoḻi, ilattīṉ, kirīkku mutaliyaṉa pōlat tamiḻ moḻiyum 'uyartaṉic cemmoḻi' yāmāṟu ciṟitu kāṭṭuvāḷ. tāṉ vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṉkaṇuḷḷa pala moḻikaṭkut talaimaiyum avaṟṟiṉum mikka mēta kavuṭaimaiyumuḷḷa moḻiyē 'uyarmoḻi. iv vilakkaṇattāṉ ārāyumiṭattut tamiḻ, teluṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kellān talaimaiyum avaṟṟiṉum mikka mētakavum uṭaimaiyāl tāṉum uyarmoḻiyē yeṉka. tāṉ vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭiṟ payilum maṟṟaiya moḻikaḷiṉ utaviyiṉṟit taṉittu iyaṅkavalla āṟṟal cāṉṟatē 'taṉimoḻi' eṉappaṭum. tāṉ piṟamoḻikaṭkuc ceyyum utavi mikuntum avai taṉakkuc ceyyum utavi kuṟaintum iruttalē vaḻakkāṟu. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉutavi kaḷaiyappaṭiṉ, teluṅku mutaliyaṉa iyaṅkutalollā; maṟṟut tamiḻ moḻi avaṟṟiṉutaviyillāmalē ciṟitu miṭarppaṭutaliṉṟit taṉittu iṉimaiyiṉ iyaṅkavallatu. iḵtu intiya moḻi nūṟpulavarkaḷ palarkkum oppa muṭintatu. ātaliṉ tamiḻ taṉimoḻiyē yeṉka. iṉic cemmoḻiyāvatu yātu? ''tiruntiya paṇpuñ cīrtta nākarikamum, poruntiya tūymoḻi pukal cemmoḻiyām" eṉpatu ilakkaṇam immoḻi nūlilakkaṇam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇṇum amaintiruttal tēṟṟam. eṉṉai? iṭarppaṭṭa coṉ muṭipukaḷum poruṇmuṭipukaḷumiṉṟic coṟṟāṉ karutiya poruḷaik kēṭṭāṉ teḷḷitiṉuṇara valla tāyp paḻaiyaṉa kaḻintu putiyaṉa pukuntu tiruttameyti niṟṟalē tiruntiya paṇpeṉappaṭuvatu. itu tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kaṇ muṟṟum amaintiruttal kāṇka. nāṭṭiṉ nākarika mutirccik kēṟpac coṟkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭup pāṣaikkum nākarika nalam viḷaittal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu coṟkaḷēṟpaṭumiṭattup piṟa pāṣaic coṟkaḷaṉṟit taṉ coṟkaḷē mikutal vēṇṭum. ivaiyum uyartaṉittamiḻ moḻikkup poruntuvaṉa vām ākavē tamiḻ tūymoḻiyumām. eṉavē tamiḻ
cemmoḻiyeṉpatu tiṇṇam itupaṟṟi yaṉṟē toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ moḻi 'centamiḻ' eṉa nallicaip pulavarāl naviṉṟōtap peṟuvatā yiṟṟu. ākavē teṉṉāṭṭiṉkaṭ ciṟantoḷirāniṉṟa amiḻtiṉumiṉiya tamiḻ moḻi evvāṟṟāṉ ārāynta vaḻiyum 'uyartaṉic cemmoḻi ' yē yām eṉpatu niccayam. ivvaḷavuyarvuñ ciṟappum vāynta arumait tamiḻmoḻiyai uṇṇāṭṭup puṉmoḻikaḷō ṭoruṅ keṇṇutal tavirttu, vaṭanāṭṭuyartaṉic cemmoḻi camaṟkirutameṉak koṇṭāṟ pōlat teṉṉāṭṭuyar taṉic cemmoḻi tamiḻeṉak koṇṭu pukutalē ēṟpuṭaittām''
3. moḻiyiyalil tamiḻiya (Tamulian) moḻikkuṭumpam eṉṟē kālṭuvel kālam varai vaḻaṅkiya peyarai avar tāṉ ''tirāviṭa moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa māṟṟi vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻ eṉpataṉ koccait tiripuvaṭivamē tirāviṭam eṉpatākum. makāvamcattil "tamiḷa", taṇṭiyiṉ eḻutiya avanticuntari kataiyil 'tramiḷa' eṉṟa collum varukiṉṟaṉa. ikṣvāku kuṭimarapiṉariṉ pirākirutak kalveṭṭilum 'tamiḷa' tāṉ varukiṟatu. piṉṉar kumārilapaṭṭariṉ tantiravārttikattil
"tatyatā tirāviṭāti pāṣāyam ēva'' (appaṭi tirāviṭa ( tamiḻ ) maṟṟum piṟa moḻikaḷil) 
eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ - tamiḷ - tramiḷa - tramiṭa-trāviṭam eṉṟu māṟiviṭṭatu. ē.ci.parṉel 1872 intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri mutal tokutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tantira vārttikap pakutiyai acciṭṭa poḻutu apattamāka
"āntra trāviṭa pāṣāyam ēva"
eṉṟu acciṭṭār (mēlum paṟpala piḻaikaḷuṭaṉ). intat tavaṟāṉa vācakattai kālṭuvel (1875) sṭeṉ kōṉō (Linguistic Survey of India: 1906) pōṉṟōr piṉpaṟṟiṉar. ittavaṟai piṭi cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār intiyaṉ āṉṭikuvāri42 ām tokutiyil (1913) teḷḷat teḷivāka niṟuviyuḷḷār. (kuñña ṇṇirājā Annals of Orental Research tokuti 28 (1979) kaṭṭuraiyil kuṟittuḷḷatu pōla parṉel uṭaiya tavaṟāṉa vācakattait tamiḻaṟiñar palarum iṉṟum piṉpaṟṟit "tamiḻaiyum, teluṅkaiyum orucērak kumārilapaṭṭar kuṟittār" eṉṟu tavaṟāka eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar)
4. caivacittānta makācamācam taṉatu 1918 mānāṭṭil ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakam tamiḻai oru cemmoḻiyāka aṟivikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīrmāṉam niṟaivēṟṟiyatu. karantait tamiḻccaṅkam 25/5/1919 - lēyē ulakac cemmoḻi kaḷiṉ mutaṉ moḻiyeṉak karutaṟkuriya aṉaittut takutikaḷaiyum tamiḻmoḻi uṭaiyatāyiruppatāl ataṉai attakaiya moḻiyāka āṭciyāḷar karuti ai.ci.es paṭṭat tiṟku atai oru pāṭamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu oru tīrmāṉamum, tamiḻ moḻiyāṉatu, toṉmaiyum cīrmaiyum cemmaiyum vāyntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyeṉap palatiṟattārālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭu vatāl, ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakattār, tām itu nāḷum koṇṭirunta koḷkaiyai māṟṟit tamiḻmoḻi ōr uyar taṉic cemmoḻiyē eṉpatai oppuk koṇṭu itteṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭup palkalaik kaḻakattil ataṟku muṟaippaṭi mutal iṭamum (First Place) ēṟpum (Recognition) tara vēṇṭum eṉṟu maṟṟoru tīrmāṉamum niṟaivēṟṟi iraṇṭu poruṇmai kaḷaiyum annāḷaiya intiya aracukkum ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattukkum aṉuppiyatu. pulavar cuntararācaṉ vaḻikāṭṭutalil pala āṇṭukaḷāka muṉaintu ceyalpaṭum talainakart tamiḻc caṅkam pōṉṟa amaippukaḷum piṟavum tamiḻaic cemmoḻi eṉa naṭuvaṇaracu aṟivikka vēṇṭumeṉa 2000 -liruntu eṭutta muyaṟcikaḷil karantait tamiḻccaṅkamum iṇaintu ceyal paṭṭatu. itu toṭarpāka tilli ceṉṟa cāliṉi iḷantiraiyaṉ 2000 - il tilliyil pēruntu mōti uyir tuṟantatu oru tuyara nikaḻvu ākum.
5. 15.03.1951 ām nāḷaṉṟu naṭaipeṟṟa intiya ilakkiyak kaḻakattiṉ (cākittiya akāṭami) toṭakka mānāṭṭil annāḷ naṭuvaṇaracuk kalvi amaiccar apulkalām ācāt pēcciṉ pakuti varumāṟu: "intiya araciyaṟ caṭṭam (tēciya moḻikaḷāka) ēṟṟuḷḷa patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷil camaṟ kirutamum tamiḻum aṭaṅkum. camaṟkirutam taṉittaṉmai vāyntatu eṉavē ciṟanta cemmoḻiyāka atu ñāyap paṭi ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻum vaḷamāṉa toṉmaiyāṉa ilak kiyaṅkaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷatu. ataṉ ceyyuḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ayal moḻikaḷil peyarkkappaṭum ciṟappu vāyntavai. tamiḻ uṇmaiyākavē ōr uyartaṉic cemmoḻi eṉpataiyum cem moḻit takuti paḻaṅkālattilēyē eytiviṭṭatu eṉpataiyum nām niṉaiviṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum"
6.1960kkup piṉṉark kaḻaka ilakkiyattil tuṟai pōyavar kaḷum ēṉaiya cemmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷai uṇarntavarkaḷum āṉa aṟiñarkaḷ kamil cuvelepil, jārj hārṭ, ē.kē. irāmāṉucaṉ ākiyōr kaḻaka ilakkiyamāṉatu piṟa cem moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum ciṟappu vāynta taṉittaṉmai vāyntatu eṉpatai viḷakkiyuḷḷa pakutikaḷ virivāka iyal 3il tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
7. aṇmaiyil cemmoḻit tamiḻ paṟṟiyum naṭuvaṇaracu tamiḻaic cemmoḻiyāka aṟivikkak kaṭanta kāl nūṟṟāṇṭākap pala iyakkaṅkaḷum piṟarum ceyta muyaṟcikaḷ paṟṟiyum veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavai vā. ce. kuḻantaicāmi (2004, 2005) aruḷi (2005) mutaliyavaiyākum. maicūriluḷḷa tamiḻc cemmoḻic ciṟappāyvu naṭuvam veḷiyiṭum kālāṇṭu itaḻ "cemmoḻiyum 1-1&2 (ak. ticampar 2006; caṉavari - mārc 2007) iṉṟu naṭantuvarum cemmoḻit tiṭṭac ceyaṟpāṭukaḷiṉ viḷattaṅkaḷaic cevvaṉē tantu varukiṉṟatu.
iyal 2kkāṉa nūṟpaṭṭiyal
Ellis, Francis Whyte (181) "Note to the introduction" in A grammar of the Teloogoo language by A.D. Campbell
Babington, Benjamin Guy (1830)An account of the sculptures and inscriptions at Mahamalaipur; Transactions of the Royal Asiatic Society Vol II (Paper read on 12 - 7 - 1828) :
“I cannot touch on the subject of ancient Tamil characters without remarking, that their extreme simplicity seems one among many circumstances, which indicate that the language is of very high antiquity. The Sanscrit of the South of India is written in characters (the Grantha) derived from the Tamil, but they are much more complicated, and therefore probably posterior in point of antiquity. The peculiar structure of the Tamil language, wholly dissimilar from the Sanserit, its deficieney in aspirated consonants, its possession of letters and sounds not found in Sanscrit, its division into dialects, one of which contains but few words of Sanscrit derivation; and sounds not found in Sanscrit, its division into dialects, and lastly, its locality at the southern extremity of India, would seem likewise to indicate an independent origin, and one of at least equal antiquity with the Sanscrit itself; but this is a subject foreign to that now under consideration, and deserving a more, lengthened discussion than the limits of a note will allow."
Hoisington, Rev Henry (1853) Brief Notes on the Tamil language. Journal of the American Oriental Society III; Article ix. (Paper read on 19 - 5 - 1852):
“No language can be more concise, copious, pliant or mellifluous than Shen Tamil”
(Tamil)“is believed to be the radix of the Telugu, the Canarese, the Malayalaim, the Tuluva and other dialects, which constitute the speech of some twenty or thirty millions of people. So that it may will be considered as occupying Southern India”
Caldwell, Robert (1856; II Edn 1875) A Comparative grammar of the Dravidian or South Indian family of languages.
Sriw at JTW 00 from 1. Chin (1903) gwin Owny varalāṟu
Meenakshisundaram, T.P. (1956) Address to Tamil section of All India oriental conference 1955 at Annamalainagar; TAMIL CULTURE V - 2 April 1956 [Reference to Abul Kalam Azad's 15.5.1951 address (“Tamil is really a classical language”]

iyal 3
cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ
(caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ) taṉicciṟappu
muccaṅkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟai mutaṉ mutalil ki.pi. 10m nūṟṟāṇṭil iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷukku urai yeḻutiya (piṟkāla) nakkīrarum, avarukkup piṉṉar cilappati kārattukku uraiyeḻutiya aṭiyārkku nallārum, kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar :
tamiḻccaṅkam 		āṇṭa pāṇṭiya 		caṅkap pulavar 	pāṭiya pulavar 	caṅkam 
maṉṉar 			eṇṇikkai 	eṇṇikkai 	ceyalpaṭṭa 
eṇṇikkai 						āṇṭukaḷ 
talaiccaṅkam		 89				549		4449 		4440 
(teṉmaturai) 
iṭaiccaṅkam 		59 				59		3700 		3700 
(kapāṭapuram) 
kaṭaiccaṅkam 		49				49		449 		1800 
(maturai = kūṭal )
197 				657 		8598		9940 
ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiru kaḻakaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyavaṟṟai varalāṟ ṟāciriyar, varalāṟṟuṇarvu uṭaiyavarkaḷ ēṟpatillai .
2. perumpālāṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum piṟa tuṟai aṟiñarkaḷum kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkattukkuc cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum maturaiyil tamiḻk kaḻakam - ataiyotta ilakkiya pīṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
"maturaiyil pāṇṭiya vēntar ātaravil cilakālam tamiḻppulavar amaippu (caṅkam) oṉṟu ceyalpaṭṭu vantatu uṇmaiyāṉa ceytiyāka irukkalām" - a. nīlakaṇṭa cāstiri.
'	'tamiḻccaṅkam iruntatu eṉṟa marapuc ceyti iṭaiviṭātu iruntu varukiṟatu. (intiyāvil ittakaiya marapuc ceyti vēṟeṅkum illai.)
tamiḻc cevvilakkiya ('caṅka'') kālattil ilak kiyaṅkaḷai vaḷarttu, taram kaṇṭu ūkkuvitta oru amaippu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatai itu kāṭṭukiṟatu" - cēviyar es. taṉināyakam.
''kaḻaka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷil taṉippāṭal kaḷāyiṉum avaṟṟiṭaiyē naṭai, coṟṟokai, yāppu, pāṭuporuḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil oṟṟumai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. 'ilakkiya amaippu cārnta taṉmai " "academism" eṉa itaik kūṟalām. akātemi pōṉṟa ilakkiya amaippu illāmal attakaiya oṟṟumai ēṟpaṭṭirukkātu. ki.pi. 1ai oṭṭi maturaiyil attakaiya akātemi, atā vatu kaḻakam iruntatu eṉa muṭivu ceyvataṟkāṉa pala piṭipāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. - kē.vi. cuvelapil Tamil literature (1974) Otto HarrassOvitz. Wiesbaden. 
ikkaruttai mēlum araṇ ceyvaṉa maturaiyil (kūṭalil) kaḻakam iruntataiyō allatu akkaḻakattiṉ ceyal pāṭṭaiyō kuṟikkum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum ākum. avai varumāṟu. 
"tamiḻ keḻu kūṭal'						 puṟam 58:13 
''tolāṇai nal āciriyar 
puṇar kūṭṭu uṇṭa pukaḻcāl ciṟappiṉ 
nilam taru tiruviṉ neṭiyōṉ”
- maturai ka. 761-3 '
'tamiḻ nilai peṟṟa tāṅkaru marapiṉ 
makiḻnaṉai marukiṉ maturai 				- ciṟupāṇ. 66 - 7 
"tamiḻ vaiyai” 						- paripāṭal 6:63 
"tamiḻ vēli" maturaiyaik kuṟittatu		 - paripāṭal 6:63

antaṇ maturait tokai ākkiṉāṉum 	- tiruñāṉacampantar

teṉmaturāpuram ceytum aṅkataṉil aruntamiḻ naṟ 
caṅkam irīit tamiḻ vaḷarttum - taḷavāypuram ceppēṭu
(parāntaka vīranārāyaṇaṉ ki.pi. 9m nū) 
"māpāratam tamiḻppaṭuttum maturāpuric caṅkam vaittatum 
periya ciṉṉamaṉūrc ceppēṭu (parāntaka vīra 
nārāyaṇaṉ makaṉ irāca cimmaṉ)
''tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal caṅkap palakaiyil amarntirunta pulavar paramparaiyaic cārnta eṭṭiccāttaṉaip paṟṟiya kuṟippu. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kalveṭṭu (ARE 334/1929-30) erukkaṅkuṭi, cāttūr vaṭṭam.

cēviyar es taṉināyakam taṉatu 'iyaṟkaiyum tamiḻc ceyyuḷum" (1996) eṉṉum nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa piṉvarum karuttu mika iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
"tamiḻc (caṅka) ceyyuḷ tamiḻakattilēyē karukkoṇṭu vaḷarntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa aṉaittuc cāṉṟukaḷaiyum koṇ ṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ pēcunar veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantu iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟō, varum poḻutē vaḷarcciyaṭainta tamiḻ moḻi, ilakkiyattai uṭaṉ koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟō kūṟuvataṟkuk kaḻaka ilakkiyam iṭam taravillai eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ.''
4. mikap paḻaiya kaḻaka nūlkaḷilēyē (tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil toṉmai cāṉṟavai) tamiḻ varivaṭivam, eḻuttumuṟai kuṟittum paṇ paṭṭa ilakkiyam kuṟittum teḷivāka, virivākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ai. makātēvaṉ (2003) karuttu , acōkaṉuṭaiya tamiḻ - pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ varivaṭivam uru vākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. ikkaruttu ēṟkattakkatāka illai. kāraṇam mēṟcoṉṉa ki.mu. 300 cārnta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ uruppeṟuvataṟkuc cila pala nūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ varivaṭivamum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. koṟkaiyil 1970il akaḻn teṭukkappaṭṭa pāṉai ōṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttiṉ kālam ki.mu. 700 eṉa appoḻutē uṟutiyākkap paṭṭatu. koṭumaṇalil aṇmaiyil akaḻnta pāṉai ōṭṭu eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ kālamum ki.mu. 500kku muntiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉa irācaṉ (2004) niṟuviyuḷḷār. āticcanallūril 2004 il naṭanta akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭa pāṉai ōṭṭup poṟippiṉ kālamum karimak kaṇippiṉpaṭi ki.mu. 700ai oṭṭiyatāka amaiya lām eṉac ceyti vantuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟaiyum piṟa cāṉṟu kaḷaiyum cuṭṭi irācaṉ (2004), naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr "tamiḻukkuk ki.mu. 800ai oṭṭiyē oru taṉi vari vaṭivam iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ataṉaip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka tamiḻ - pirāmi varivaṭivam (lipi) ki.mu. 300 il uruvākkap paṭṭirukkum" eṉpataṟkāṉa valuvāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷai muṉ vaittuḷḷaṉar. cintuveḷi nākarika varivaṭivam tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatu āṉapaṭiyāl pirāmi varivaṭivamum atiliruntē uruvākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar hīrās (New Review; cūlai 1936). tamatu paṇṭait tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ 1938 nūlil ti.nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ kūṟiyataiyum niṉaivu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
"pirāmi lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟ paṭavillaiyeṉṟum uyir eḻuttukkaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappaiyaḷippatum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ varkka eḻuttukkaḷaik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣaikkeṉa amaik kappaṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭum paṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ pirāmi mutalil tamiḻukkeṉa amainta lipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu'' 
vaṭanāṭṭu varalāṟṟaṟiñar es. ār. kōyal (1979) karuttum acōka pirāmi eḻuttu paṭippaṭiyāka (vaṭanāṭṭil) uruvāṉa taṉṟu; atu oru kāla kaṭṭattil (ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 300 eṉak kūṟalām) putitāka uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum.
5. tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta ki.mu. 1900 - ki.mu. 300 cārnta peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ toṭarcciyē kaḻaka ilakkiya kālam; annākarikak kūṟukaḷil perumpālāṉavai kaḻaka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum irācaṉ pōṉṟavar kaḷuṭaiya aṭutta muṭivākum. āka ivvārāyccikaḷ mēlum muṉṉēṟum poḻutu tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300kkum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa aṉaivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppuḷḷatu.
6. ittaṟuvāyil iṉṉoṉṟaiyum kūṟa vēṇṭum. ki.mu. 300kku muṟpaṭṭa kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ evaiyumē namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai . ki.mu. 300 - kipi. 300 kālakaṭṭattaic cārnta kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum muḻumaiyāka namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai. ki.pi. 300ai oṭṭik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷait tokuttavarkaḷ evvevaṟṟai viṭṭu vaikkalām eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷō avaṟṟai maṭṭum tokuttu viṭṭu maṟṟavaṟṟai nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. tokuttavaṟṟilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytirukkalām. nuṇṇaṟi vuṭaiyavarkaḷ itupaṟṟi 1877-liruntu terivittuḷḷavaṟṟaik kāṇpōm : -
cārlas ī kōvar : teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭal kaḷ (The Folk Songs of Southern India) 1871: tiṭṭa miṭṭuc citaikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟi yeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅ kālat tamiḻ nūl etaiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattaik kaiviṭṭut toṉma (purāṇakkataikaḷai nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻaippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattaic citaittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷilum annūlkaḷai kayavāḷit taṉamākac citaittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūlanūl kaḷiṉ karuttai uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. civattampi (1986yuem ikkaruttiṉarē.
hayūnevil : ti tapropēṉiyaṉ (The Taprobanian) cūṉ 1896 "maturait tamiḻk kaḻakamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkap paṭṭapoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷkai vāṭai irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattil makāvamcam iruntatupōl pāṇṭi yarkaḷ varalāṟṟaik kūṟum oru nūl maturaik kaḻaka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka irunta aḻikkappaṭṭu ataṟku māṟṟāka tiruviḷai yāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum. aḻikkap paṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citaivukaḷuṭaṉ, caṅkamarkaḷ (pārp paṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ ) eṇṇam, camayam ākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉaikataikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇaintu ap purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu".
jāṉ spiyars "ñāṉam tōṉṟiya iṭattiliruntu" vālyūs Values II - 10 cūlai 1957 : "cintuveḷi nākarika muttiraikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (toltamiḻ) vācakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal koṇṭa (aḻintupōṉa) paṇṭai nūlkaḷaip paṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum puttar kālamākiya ki.mu. 600kku muntiya nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟukūṭa kiṭaik kātataṟ kāṉa kāraṇattai nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. (tol tamiḻaruṭaiya) paṇṭaiya āvaṇaṅkaḷai āriyarkaḷ oṉṟu viṭāmal aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatē atu.
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉ : tolkāppiyam - collatikāram : kallāṭar urai (1971) : "tolkāppiyattai ataṉ āciriyar enta uruvattil viṭṭuc ceṉṟārō anta vaṭivattil atu namakku vantu cēra villai" (tolkāppiyam : poruḷatikāram marapiyalil pala iṭaic cerukalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu tolkāppiya aṟiñarkaḷ ti. cavarirāyaṉ, kā.cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷai, cōmacuntara pārati, veḷḷaivāraṇaṉ, ilakkuvaṉār, kamil cuvelapil, irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ, ce.vai. caṇmukam pōṉṟavar ēṟ ṟuḷḷatākum. kuṟippāka nālvaraṇam paṟṟik kūṟum marapiyal nūṟpākkaḷ 71-85 iṭaic cerukal eṉpatu ivvaṟiñarkaḷum (ēṉ naṭunilaiyāḷar aṉaivarum) ēṟpatākum.

tolkāppiyam
7. tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varai eṉpataip perumpālāṉa naṭunilai aṟiñar ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. camaṟkirutattukku (pāṣaikku) ilakkaṇam varainta pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭu. pāṇiṉikku muntiya camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. vaṭa intiyāviṟkuḷ nākarikamaṟṟa āriyar nuḻainta ki.mu.1500kku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil intiyāveṅkum tamiḻiya moḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟukku varivaṭivamum iruntatu. ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum iruntu avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum karuttukkaḷum irukku vētattilum ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu uṇmai. eṉavē tolkāppiyamō allatu ataṟku muntiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷō " eṉpa" "eṉmaṉār pulavar" pōṉṟavai ivaṟṟaic cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa) pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭavaiyāka irun tirukkalām eṉac cila tamiḻaṟiñar karutuvatu cāṉṟillāta kūṟṟaṉṟu. "tolkāppiyar eḻutiyataṉāl "tolkāppiyam" eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭu varum marapu. āyiṉum tolkāppiyam valla aṟiñarkaḷ, ilakkuvaṉār, ca.vē.cuppiramaṇiyaṉ, poṟkō, culevapil pōṉṟavarkaḷ "tol + kāppu + iyam" - tolkāppiyam, atāvatu toṉmarapaik kākkum nūl eṉak kāraṇap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatum poruttamākavē uḷḷatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttu, col atikāraṅkaḷilum poruḷ atikāra akattiṇai, puṟattiṇai iyalkaḷilum cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭaic cerukalkaḷ piṟkālattil ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatum poruḷatikārattiṉ iṟuti ēḻu iyalkaḷilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭaiccerukal mikappala eṉpatum cuvelapil, mār pōṉṟōr karuttu. pirāmaṇiya ātikkam mikunta kālattil tolkāppiyam muḻumaiyāka aḻittoḻikkappaṭāmal pātukāppataṟkeṉṟē tamiḻ, tamiḻar pāl paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷum cila iṭaiccerukalkaḷai virakākac ceytirukkalām eṉpar pāvāṇar. (eṉiṉum tolkāppiya mutaluraikārar iḷam pūraṇar kālamākiya ki.pi. 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarc ceytu muṭikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum) tolkāppiyam "māntaṉ aṟivāṟṟal evvaḷavu viyattaku ucca nilai eytakkūṭum eṉpataik kāṭṭum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu' One of the finest monuments of human intelligence TUI eṉpar cuvelapil. ilakkiyak koḷkaiyil vēṟu entap paṇṭaiya moḻikaḷilum illāta ciṟanta karuttukkaḷait tol poruḷ. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟuvatai cuvelapil (1991) kaṭṭurai viḷakkukiṟatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattil kaṭai āṟu iyalkaḷum puṇarcci paṟṟiyaṉa. "tamiḻil puṇarcci paṟṟiya ilakkaṇam taṉittaṉmai koṇṭatu. oṭṭunilai viṭṭut taḷarātu, iṉṟaḷavum ''kaṉṉit tamiḻāka" viḷaṅkuvatu, ippuṇarcci paṟṟiya aṟiviyal aṇuku muṟaikaḷiṉālēyām'' (tamiḻaṇṇa l, 2004)
tolkāppiyac ceyyuḷiyal kōṭpāṭṭuc ciṟappup paṟṟi cuvelepil (1974) kūṟiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
tolkāppiyam aṭippaṭai nūlāka iruppatu ilak kaṇa ilakkiya āyvukaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟu; mānta iṉap paraval cārnta puviyiyal, kumukāya māntaviyal, paṇpāṭu, cūḻnilaiyiyal, uḷaviyal ākiya pulaṅkaḷ cārnta ariya ceytikaḷ annūlil uḷḷaṉa ; eṉavē tamiḻar nākarikam - paṇpāṭu cārnta (ēṉ potuvāṉa mānta iṉa nākarikam - paṇpāṭu cārnta) āyvukaḷukkut tolkāppiyam mika iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. eṉiṉum ilakkiyam kūṟuvatellām (akkālattil nilaviya) uṇmaik kumukāya nilaiyai nēraṭiyākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal tavaṟu; uṇmai nilaiyai viruppu veṟuppiṉṟi aṟiviyal pūrvamākak kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇutal mēlum tavaṟākum. akkāla āṇ peṇmakkaḷiṉ akam, puṟa vāḻkkaiyai uḷḷatu uḷḷapaṭi tatrupamākac caṅkap pulavarkaḷuṭaiya pāṭalkaḷ kāṭṭuvatākak kumuka varalāṟṟāciriyar cilar muṉṉar koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl uṇmai eṉṉa? ceyyuḷ 'uṇmai vāḻkkai" nikaḻccikaḷai antanta nikaḻcci eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa ilakkiya marapu uḷḷatō anta marapukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭē cittirikkiṟatu. atāvatu marapuk kēṟṟa māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ cittirikkiṟatu. ''uṇmai vāḻkkai " vēṟu; atai ilakkiyattil kāṭṭum marapu vēṟu; eṉpataip pulavarkaḷum poruḷilakkaṇavāciriyarum uṇarn tiruntaṉar. uṇarntiruntataṉāltāṉ uṇmai vāḻkkaiyiṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ ceykaikaḷai 'ulakiyal vaḻakku" eṉṟum ilakkiyattil (ceyyuḷil) vāḻkkaiyaic cittirikkum marapaic ceyyuḷ vaḻakku "pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku" eṉṟum kuṟittaṉar. cila nikaḻccikaḷ, ceytikaḷai ippaṭi ippaṭit tāṉ kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ilakkiyap paṭaippāḷarkaḷum vācakarkaḷum tamakkuḷ icaintu uruvākkum mēlvaric caṭṭamākiya marapē ilakkiya marapākum. (e.kā. ovvoru tiṇaikkum uriya mutal, karu, uripporuḷkaḷ). nikaḻcci / ceyti vācakarukkut terintatu tāṉ; ataṉai oru putiya pārvaiyil, kōṇattil (marapai aṭippaṭaiyil mīṟāmal) kāṭṭum poḻutu vācakar anta nayattai naṉku uṇarntu pārāṭṭa iyalkiṟatu. (itaṉai pi.marutanāyakam tamatu Poetics of the lyrics in classical languages" (Chemmozhi 1-2 Jan - March 2007) kaṭṭuraiyil mēlum teḷivākkukiṟār) akappāṭal etuvum orutaṭavai kūṭat talaivaṉ, talaivi peyaraic cuṭṭu vatillai. talaivaṉum talaiviyum eppoḻutum iṉṉār eṉṟu kuṟippāka aṭaiyāḷam kāṭṭa muṭiyātavarkaḷē. eṉavē akappāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum uṇarvukaḷ (ṭapiḷyu. ec. haṭcaṉ tamatu "ilakkiyap payiṟcikkāṉa muṉṉurai' 1946 nūl pakkam 97 il colvatu pōla) "potuvāka enta āṭavaṉukkum peṇṇukkum poruntuvatāka irukku mēyaṉṟi ivaṉukkut tāṉ ivaḷukkuttāṉ poruntum eṉṟu irukkātu. (uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷai viḷakkātu) veḷiyulakai viritturaikkum puṟappāṭalkaḷil cila potuvāka aṉaivarum nōkkum pārvaiyil irukkum; cila antantap pulavaruṭaiya taṉi nōkkil amaintatāka irukkum. piṉṉavaṟṟukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kaiyaṟunilaip pāṭalkaḷ. vaḷḷalkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ iṟantāl pulavarkaḷ tam neñciṉ āḻattiliruntu uṇarvukaḷaik koṭṭip pāṭum tuyarap pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉittaṉmai vāyntavai; potu vāṉa nāṭṭuppuṟa oppārikaḷiṉ vālāyap paṭimaṅkaḷaik koṇṭavaiyalla".
8. tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyattiṉ mukāmai nūl kaḷākiya eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ (vēṟu entac cemmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta) ciṟappukaḷāka kamil cuvelapil, jārj lūcarṉ hārṭ, ēkē. rāmāṉucaṉ pi. maruta nāyakam ākiyōr terivittuḷḷa karuttukkaḷ cila varumāṟu. (innālvarumē kaḻaka ilakkiyattai āḻntu payiṉṟavarkaḷ; piṟa cemmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum terintavarkaḷ ; nuṇmāṇuḻaipula mikkavarkaḷ) :
"eṭṭuttokai, pattuppāṭṭu nūlkaḷ (mottam 2300 pāṭalkaḷ) acciṭṭu ulakiṉ pārvaikku mutalil vantatu 1880 - 1910 kāla aḷavil ākum. avai veḷivanta akkaṇamē (atuvarai eḷitāṉa piṟkāla intiya moḻikaḷil oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭa nilai māṟi ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil oṉṟākat tamiḻ ākiviṭṭatu. pireñcu āciyaviyalaṟiñar piyar meyl "kirēkka uṇarccip pākkaḷil talai ciṟanta navamaṇikaḷukku iṇaiyāṉavai iccaṅka ilakkiyap pāṭal kaḷ; intiyāvil, ēṉ ulakil, ilakkiyap paṭaippukaḷiṉ cikaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu caṅka ilakkiyam" eṉṟu ceytuḷḷa matippīṭṭai caṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷai āḻntu payi ṉṟuḷḷa evarum ēṟṟuk koḷvar.''
- cuvelapil (1960) 
"ulakiṉ nākarikam - paṇpāṭṭukkut tirāviṭarkaḷ, kuṟip pākat tamiḻarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa ciṟanta paṅkaḷippukaḷ pallava, cōḻar kālak kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭakkalai ; cōḻar kālac ceppup paṭimaṅkaḷ ; parata nāṭṭiyam eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum tamiḻar naṭaṉak kalai ; karunāṭaka icai eṉa aḻaikkappaṭum tamiḻ icai mutaliya palavām; avaṟṟuḷḷum talaimai cāṉṟa ciṟappu vāyntatu (caṅka kālac cemmoḻit tamiḻ ilakkiyam. piṟamoḻi, piṟa nāṭṭu aṟiñar itai uṇarntu ivvilakki yattaip payiṉṟu ulaka mānta iṉattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta uyariya marapuc celvamāka makiḻntu pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. uccanilaikku muḻut takutiyuṭaiya avvilakkiyattiṟku itu varai annilai tarappaṭavillai .''
- cuvelapil (1973) 
"intiyāviṉ piṟa enta moḻi ilakkiyattilum illāta ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷaik koṇṭatu eṉpatu maṭṭumaṉṟip paṇṭait tamiḻ (caṅka) ilakkiyam uyariya ilakkiya nayamum vāyntatu. ulakiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷ varicaiyil tamiḻukkuriya iṭattaik kaḻaka ilakkiyattiṉ 26350 varikaḷ koṇṭa pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟut tantuviṭṭaṉa. av vilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappai ippoḻututāṉ ulaku uṇarat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.''
''acai, cīr, taḷai aṭippaṭaiyilāṉa yāppiyal, akam puṟak kōṭpāṭu; uvamaviyal, ākiya aṉaittilum taṉit taṉmai vāynta ciṟanta ilakkiyak koḷkai koṇṭa kaḻaka nūlkaḷai uruvākkiya perumai tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭukku maṭṭumē uriya ciṟappākum.
"intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta cevviyal ilakkiyam - cevviyal moḻip perum paṇpāṭukaḷ iraṇṭu - oṉṟu camaṟkirutam; maṟṟatu tamiḻ ; avai oṉṟai oṉṟu cārntavai yalla - taṟcārpāṉavai.
"intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyē tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē cevvilakkiyamākavum, nikaḻkāla ilakkiyamākavum orē nērattil ilaṅkum ciṟappu vāyntatu; atu camaṟkirutac cevviyal ilakkiyam aḷavu toṉmai vāyntatu; paṇṭai kirēkka moḻic ceyyuḷkaḷai eppaṭic cevvilakkiyam eṉa ulaku karutukiṟatō atē taṉmai vāyntatu tamiḻk kaḻaka ilakkiyam. uyirt tuṭippuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum uruvākum moḻiyākavum tamiḻ uḷḷatu.

"tamiḻc cevvilakkiyam (caṅka ilakkiyam) makkaḷaip paṟṟiya ilakkiyam ; makkaḷ uruvākkiyatu - āṉāl "nāṭṭup puṟa ilakkiyamaṉṟu"
- cuvelapil (1973) 
''vāḻkkai iṉiyatu; makiḻcci taruvatu. cāvu el lōrukkum varuvatu. ivvulaka vāḻviliruntu viṭutalai peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa muyaṟciyellām illai. vaḷḷaṇmai yum naṉmaiyum iyalpāka amaikiṉṟaṉa - ātaṉiya (āṉmīka) nalaṉukkākavō kaḻuvāyākavō alla. iṟantapiṉṉar vīrarum cāṉṟōrum tuṟakkattil nilaiyāka vāḻvar; tīyōr mīḷā nirayam eytuvar eṉak karutiṉar. nila ulakil pukaḻai niṟīic ceṉṟavar tuṟakkattil iṉiya vāḻvu eṉṉum vekumati peṟuvar : kātal, puṇarcci, tirumaṇam, makkaṭpēṟu ivai mānta vāḻvil niṟaivu peṟuvataṟkāṉa vaḻimuṟaikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭṭaṉa. vaḷḷaṉmai, vīracceyal, piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉāka vāḻtal - ivaṟṟil oruvaṉ celaviṭṭa nāṭkaḷai niṉaittup pulavarum piṟarum kūṟum pukaḻ moḻikaḷ maṭṭumē oruvaṉ cāvukkup piṉṉar nilainiṟpavaiyākum.''
- cuvelapil (1974) 
''kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ekkālattukkum poruntum aḻakiyal kūṟukaḷ, viḻumaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai nirampak koṇ ṭavai. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar yātta avvilakkiyaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil (atāvatu aṇmaikkālat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar) vakuttuḷḷa ilakkiya nayakkōṭpāṭukaḷiṉ paṭiyum uyar nilaiyil ēṟkappaṭuvaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa.
''kaḻaka ilakkiyattaip payilum oru kumukāyamē (taṉinapar maṭṭumalla) avvilakkiya viḻumaṅkaḷai eyta iyalum vakaiyil, eyta viḻaiyum vakaiyil avvilakkiyam amain tuḷḷatu. eṉavē tāṉ ikkāla ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvāḷarum pōṟṟum vakaiyil ulaka māntar aṉaivarum matikkat takka eṉṟu muḷ (cirañcīvi)ttaṉmai koṇṭuḷḷatu kaḻaka ilakkiyam''
- cuvelapil (1974) 

"paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyamum paṇpāṭum paṭaitta akam - puṟakkōṭpāṭukaḷ ulakilēyē taṉittaṉmaiyum ciṟap pum vāyntavai. cila poruṇmaikaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattukkum camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattukkum oppumai kāṇappaṭalām. paṭṭāl eṉṉa? camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiliruntu tāṉ tamiḻ ilakkiyam kaṭaṉ peṟṟirukkum eṉa ēṉ karuta vēṇṭum? (nērmāṟākavum nikaḻntirukkalām aṉṟō ?)"
- cuvelapil (1975) 
"potuvāṉa ceytiyait teḷivāka, muḻumaiyāṉa nuṇṇiya pārvaiyil nōkki, eḷiya āṉāl vayiram pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil, āḻnta poruḷuṭaṉ taruvatē ciṟanta pāṭal eṉa ikkālattil karutappaṭukiṟatu. veḷippaṭaiyāṉa orē ceytiyait taruvataiviṭa aṭukkaṭukkāṉa pala poruḷait taravallatāṉa coṟceṭṭumikka pāṭalē ciṟanta pālat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. tolkāppiyam nōkku, payaṉ, eccam (colleccam, kuṟippeccam) uḷḷuṟai, iṟaicci eṉṟu kūṟuvatellām itu tāṉ ;
- pi. marutanāyakam (2007) 
9. iṟutiyākac cevviyal moḻikaḷāṉa tamiḻ, camaṟkirutam, kirēkkam, ilattīṉ ākiya nāṉkum terintavarum - am moḻikaḷiṉ cevviyal ilakkiyaṅkaḷaik kaṟṟavarumāṉa jārj lūcarṉ hārṭ tamiḻ cemmoḻi eṉpataṟkāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷaip piṉvarumāṟu 2003il ōr aṟikkaiyil terivittuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu :
''mutalāvatāka, tamiḻiṉ toṉmai kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ikkālattiya piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ōrāyiram āṇṭu muṉṉar uruvāṉa ilakkiyattaik koṇṭatu tamiḻ. tamiḻiṉ āti nūl ākiya tolkāppiyattiṉ pakutikaḷ ki.mu. 200ai oṭṭiyavai eṉpatu mikap paḻaiya (tamiḻil) kal veṭṭukaḷiliruntu terikiṟatu. paḻantamiḻp perunūlkaḷāṉa eṭṭuttokai pattuppāṭṭu mutaliyavai ki.pi oṉṟu - iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷaic cārntavai. intiyāviṉ miku toṉmai vāynta , camayam cārāta (secular) pāṭalkaḷ avaiyē; kāḷitācaṉ ilacciyaṅkaḷukku irunūṟu āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭavai.''
"iraṇṭāvatāka, tamiḻ ilakkiya marapu maṭṭum tāṉ (camaṟkirutattiliruntu peṟappaṭāta) intiya maṇṇukkuriya ilakkiya marapu ākum. teṉṉāṭṭil camaṟkirutat tākkam valuppaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē mukiḻttatu atu. camaṟkiruta piṟa intiya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa taṉmai yuṭaiyatu atu. tamiḻ taṉakkeṉṟu taṉi ilakkiyak koḷkai, ilakkaṇa marapu, murukiyal (aesthetics) uṭaiyatu; ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil amainta tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam vēṟu emmoḻi ilakkiyattukkum illāta taṉittaṉmai vāyntatu (unique). camaṟkirutattilō vēṟu intiya moḻi kaḷilō uḷḷa uṇarvukaḷiliruntu māṟupaṭṭa uṇarvai (sensibility) - intiya maṇṇukkē uriya uṇarvaip paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil kāṇalām; tamiḻppulavarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅkālam vaḷartteṭutta ceḻumaiyāṉa uṇarvu marapunilai atu.
"mūṉṟāvatāka, camaṟkirutam - ilattīṉ, cīṉam, pāracīkam, arapu ākiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ciṟantavaṟṟukku iṇaiyāṉa taram vāyntatu tamiḻc cemmoḻi ilakkiyam. avvilakkiyam nuṇmāṇuḻaipulamikkatu; palvēṟu pāṭu poruḷ koṇṭatu (muṟkāla intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil potu makkaḷaip paṟṟiyum niṟaiyap pāṭiyatu tamiḻilakkiyam maṭṭumē); mānta iṉattukkup potumaiyāṉa viḻumiyaṅkaḷaik kūṟiyatu. eṉavē piṟa cevviyal moḻikaḷiṉ marapukaḷuṭaṉum ilakkiyaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum camamāka niṟkum uraṉ vāyntatu atu. ulakiṉ talaiciṟanta aṟanūlkaḷil oṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷ. tamiḻc cevviyal ilakkiyattil atu pōṉṟu pala tuṟaikaḷilum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. avvilakkiyam pāṭit tulakkam tarāta mānta iṉa paṭṭaṟivu oṉṟum illai.
"iṟutiyāka ikkāla intiyap paṇpāṭṭaiyum marapaiyum uṇarva taṟkup payila vēṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil tamiḻilakkiyam mutaṉmai vāyntatu. camaṟkiruta ilakkiyaṅkaḷil teṉṉaka maraput tākkam uḷḷatu kuṟittu nāṉ virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. kaḻakakkālam toṭaṅkit tamiḻilil pāṭappaṭṭa ciṟanta intu camaya patti ilakkiya nūlkaḷ tām taṟpōtaiya intu camayattukku aṭittaḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ patti ilakkiyak karuttukaḷ pākavata purāṇattilum piṟa (teluṅku kaṉṉapcamaṟkiruta) patti nūlkaḷilum ēṟṟappaṭṭu intiyā muḻuvatum paraviṉa. vētattukkuc camamākak karutap paṭum tamiḻ (tivyapirapantap patti ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvil periya tirumāl kōyilkaḷil (tiruppati pōṉṟavai) vētattōṭu cērttu ōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāla intō āriya moḻikaḷukku camaṟkirutamē mūlamoḻi; atu pōla ikkālat tamiḻukkum malaiyāḷattukkum cevviyal tamiḻē mūla moḻiyākum.
''ulakattiṉ ciṟanta cemmoḻikaḷil tamiḻum oṉṟu eṉpatu ittuṟaiyil valla aṟiñarukku uḷḷaṅkai nellik kaṉi pōlat terinta uṇmaiyākum" 
caṅka kāla akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ
10. caṅka ilakkiya akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷiṉ taṉic ciṟappai ē. kē. rāmāṉucaṉ (1969) pārāṭṭiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
"toṉmaiyāṉavaiyē eṉiṉum iṉṟum uyirūṭṭam uḷḷavaiyāy ilaṅkum inta (caṅka ilakkiya) akap pāṭal kaḷaip pōṉṟa nayam vāyntavai intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu evaṟṟilum illai. appāṭalkaḷ kūṟum vāḻkkai neṟi yilum, viḷakkum nikaḻccikaḷilum ciṟanta cemmoḻi ilak kiyattiṉ piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa : kātalōṭu kaṉi vum paṇpāṭum ; veḷippaṭaik kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu uḷḷuṟai, iṟaicci, aṅkatam ākiyavaiyum; talaivaṉ, talaivi peyar cuṭṭappaṭāviṭiṉum ōviyam pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya vaṇṇaṉai; aṭikaḷ cila avai cuṭṭum poruḷō peritu. tamiḻar aṟivuttiṟaṉiṉ mikat toṉmaiyāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu iv vakappāṭalkaḷ ; atu maṭṭumaṉṟu kaṭanta īrāyiram āṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyap paṭaippil ivaṟṟai viñcuvaṉa illai."
11. akap pāṭalkaḷiṉ amaippup paṟṟi cuvelapil (1986) viḷakkamum karuttiṟ koḷḷat takkatu.
"akappāṭal ovvoṉṟum oru kūṟṟu : talaivaṉ, talaivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliya cilaruḷ oruvar, nāṭaka māntar pōl, kūṟuvatāka atu amaiyum. avarkaḷ oruvarukkoruvarō, taṉakkut tāṉākavō, nilavai nōkkiyō kūṟuvatai vācakar kaḷākiya nām kēṭkiṟōm; avvaḷavē. nāṭakak kāṭci oṉṟai muḻumaiyāka akappāṭal oṉṟil uḷḷa kūṟṟu viḷakkik kāṭṭiviṭum. ivai nāṭṭuppāṭalkaḷ pōl viraintu pāṭiyavai alla; oru nikaḻcciyaik kaṇṭa allatu eṇṇip pārtta oru pāvalar atu paṟṟiya tam eṇṇaṅkaḷai nērākavō urumāṟṟiyō veḷippaṭuttiya pāṭalkaḷum - alla. ivai aracavaippāṭalkaḷiṉ naḷiṉam uṭaiyavai; ''vaikalum eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ tiṅkaḷ valitta kāl pōl" nuṇṇiya pulamaittiṟaṉuṭaṉ yākkappaṭṭavai.
12. mēṟkaṇṭa iruvar kūṟṟukkaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ varumāṟu:
“In their antiquity and in their contemporaniety, there is not much else in any Indian literature equal to these quiet and dramatic Tamil poems. In their values and stances they represent a mature classical poetry : passion is balanced by courtesy, transparency by ironies and nuances of design, impersonality by vivid detail, leanness of line by richness of implication. These poems are not just the earliest evidence of the Tamil genius. The Tamils in all their 2000 years of literary effort wrote nothing better”. - A.K. Ramanujan.
An Aham poem is always a “Kurru”, an utterance by one of the limited number of dramatis personale, (hero, heroine, heroine's girl-friend, foster mother ete). The reader only overhears what the characters say to each other, to themselves, or to the moon. A poem in this tradition implies, evokes, enacts a drama in a monologue. The poems are not the result of rapid composition like oral epics or as lyrical expessions or sublimations of the poet's own feelings triggered by some romantic event. They are the result of skillful and subtle care, the sophistication of court poetry, “like a chariot wheel made thoughtfully over a month by a carpenter who tosses off eight chariots in a day.'' - K.V. Zvelebil.
